📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 2025年1月至2026年4月,美国确诊了4102例麻疹病例,其中92.5%的病例未接种疫苗或疫苗接种状态未知;23.7%的病例为5岁以下儿童,47.4%为5-19岁儿童。 [10.1111/josh.70202]。
  • 对 77 名患者的分析表明,超过 2 年的生存期是可以实现的,但存在差异。生存分布显示,44.2% 的生存时间为 2 至 <5 年,28.6% 的生存时间为 5 至 <10 年,27.3% 的生存时间≥10 年。尽管 62.3% 的患者最初获得缓解或稳定,但 31.2% 的患者后来出现病情进展、严重残疾或死亡。报告的与延长生存期相关的因素包括 治疗 反应性 (62.3%)、疾病进展缓慢 (24.7%) 和复发缓解或双相模式 (20.8%)。其他影响因素包括 宿主 相关因素 (18.2%)、成人发病疾病 (10.4%)、早期 诊断 和脑电图改善。在 31 项 队列研究 研究中,总生存率仍然很低。
  • 大多数 麻疹 患者是 患病率 合并症和并发症较高的婴儿,并且大量患者的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围不足。系统发育分析揭示了与俄罗斯和美国流行的 B3 菌株的相关性,强调了全球传播。氨基酸取代和结构分析强调了当前分离株与参考菌株相比的微小结构变化。
  • 医护人员中74.9%的血清流行率免疫阳性,25.1%不阳性,均报告过疫苗接种,年龄与IgG水平和免疫状态呈显着正相关,年龄每增加一岁,免疫阳性的几率增加14.3%,与性别无显着相关。 [10.1111/tmi.70207]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • From January 2025 to April 2026, there were 4,102 diagnosed measles cases in the United States, with 92.5% of cases being unvaccinated or having unknown vaccination status; 23.7% of cases were in children under 5 years, and 47.4% in those aged 5–19.
  • The estimated number susceptible in Israel on 1 March 2018 ranged from 0.55 million (6.3%) to 1.92 million (22.4%), with a central estimate of 1.22 million (14.2%).
  • Analysis of 77 patients demonstrated that survival beyond 2 years is achievable but variable. Survival distribution showed 44.2% surviving 2 to <5 years, 28.6% surviving 5 to <10 years, and 27.3% surviving ≥10 years.
  • Most of the measles patients were infants with a high prevalence of comorbidity and complications, and significant number of patients had inadequate vaccination coverage.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: medRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-29  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.07.28.26359095  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

从 血清流行率 到 麻疹 暴发 风险:多国流行病学概念证明

From Seroprevalence to Measles Outbreak Risk: A Multicountry Epidemiological Proof of Concept
作者： Eran Kopel, Ravit Bassal
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高国家 疫苗接种 覆盖率可能掩盖了特定年龄和空间集中的 麻疹 易感性。该研究旨在评估已发表的年龄特异性血清阳性结果是否可以转换为与后续 麻疹 发病率 相对应的随时间更新的敏感性概况。
设计与对象
该研究是使用以色列、荷兰、捷克和澳大利亚已发表的血清阳性数据进行的多国流行病学概念验证。对于以色列,2015 年特定年龄血清阳性率估计值被映射到月出生率 队列研究 并预计到 2018 年 3 月 1 日。
核心方法
已发布的血清阳性估计值被映射到每月出生 队列研究 并随着时间的推移进行预测,考虑了出生、衰老、母源抗体、常规 疫苗接种、疫苗 有效性和测定不确定性。外部比较使用一个通用框架,涵盖年龄分布、分析分类、疫苗接种、输入、空间集中和 传播 背景。
主要结果
The estimated number susceptible in Israel on 1 March 2018 ranged from 0.55 million (6.3%) to 1.92 million (22.4%), with a central estimate of 1.22 million (14.2%). Children aged <1 year had the highest central susceptible proportion (75.7%) and incidence (196.0 per 100,000). Jerusalem District accounted for 2,202 of 4,311 national cases, with susceptibility concentrated in communities with lower first-dose coverage.
解释与创新
公布的血清阳性可以识别免疫差距,但有用​​的 暴发 风险评估还必须代表易感密度、引入压力和当地 传播 条件。该模型确定了儿童早期的显着易感性,支持以色列将第二次 MMRV 剂量从 6 岁提前到 18 个月的决定。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过强调特定年龄的易感性热点和空间覆盖的重要性,为有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略提供信息。他们证明,血清流行率 数据在正确建模时可以预测 暴发 风险并指导政策调整以减少高风险群体中的 传播 窗口。
局限与证据强度
该模型并非旨在直接比较替代的 疫苗接种 计划,外部比较受到不同数据质量和 暴发 上下文的限制。证据强度中等,因为该方法在生物学上是合理的,并且针对观察到的 暴发 进行了验证,但依赖于有关 疫苗 有效性和测定准确性的假设。

From Seroprevalence to Measles Outbreak Risk: A Multicountry Epidemiological Proof of Concept

Authors: Eran Kopel, Ravit Bassal
Original abstract (English)
Background: High national vaccination coverage may conceal age-specific and spatially concentrated measles susceptibility. Objective: To assess whether published age-specific seropositivity results can be converted into a time-updated susceptibility profile that corresponds with subsequent measles incidence, while distinguishing susceptibility from infectious introductions and transmission conditions. Methods: For Israel, published 2015 age-specific seropositivity estimates were mapped to monthly birth cohorts and projected to 1 March 2018, accounting for births, aging, maternal antibody, routine vaccination, vaccine effectiveness, and uncertainty in assay interpretation. The primary outcome was reported age-specific incidence during the 2018-2019 outbreak; national and Jerusalem District case burdens were secondary outcomes. Published evidence from the Netherlands, Czechia, and Australia was compared using a common framework covering age distribution, assay classification, vaccination, importation, spatial concentration, and transmission context. Results: The estimated number susceptible in Israel on 1 March 2018 ranged from approximately 0.55 million (6.3% of the modelled population) to 1.92 million (22.4%), with a central estimate of 1.22 million (14.2%). Children aged <1 year had the highest central susceptible proportion (75.7%) and the highest later incidence (196.0 per 100,000). Jerusalem District accounted for 2,202 of 4,311 reported national cases, consistent with susceptibility concentrated in communities with lower first-dose coverage. The external comparisons showed that clustering amplified Dutch outbreak risk, survey design affected Czech estimates, and importation dominated Australian activity. Conclusions: Published seropositivity can identify immunity gaps, but useful outbreak-risk assessment must also represent susceptible density and distribution, introduction pressure, and local transmission conditions. Although the model was not designed to compare alternative vaccination schedules directly, its identification of substantial susceptibility during early childhood provides epidemiological support for Israel's recent decision to advance the second routine MMRV dose from 6 years to 18 months of age, thereby shortening the period during which young children remain dependent on single-dose protection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High national vaccination coverage may conceal age-specific and spatially concentrated measles susceptibility. The study aimed to assess whether published age-specific seropositivity results can be converted into a time-updated susceptibility profile that corresponds with subsequent measles incidence.
Design and population
The study was a multicountry epidemiological proof of concept using published seropositivity data from Israel, the Netherlands, Czechia, and Australia. For Israel, 2015 age-specific seropositivity estimates were mapped to monthly birth cohorts and projected to 1 March 2018.
Core methods
Published seropositivity estimates were mapped to monthly birth cohorts and projected over time, accounting for births, aging, maternal antibody, routine vaccination, vaccine effectiveness, and assay uncertainty. External comparisons used a common framework covering age distribution, assay classification, vaccination, importation, spatial concentration, and transmission context.
Main results
The estimated number susceptible in Israel on 1 March 2018 ranged from 0.55 million (6.3%) to 1.92 million (22.4%), with a central estimate of 1.22 million (14.2%). Children aged <1 year had the highest central susceptible proportion (75.7%) and incidence (196.0 per 100,000). Jerusalem District accounted for 2,202 of 4,311 national cases, with susceptibility concentrated in communities with lower first-dose coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
Published seropositivity can identify immunity gaps, but useful outbreak-risk assessment must also represent susceptible density, introduction pressure, and local transmission conditions. The model identified substantial susceptibility during early childhood, supporting Israel's decision to advance the second MMRV dose from 6 years to 18 months.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform targeted vaccination strategies by highlighting age-specific susceptibility hotspots and the importance of spatial coverage. They demonstrate that seroprevalence data, when properly modeled, can predict outbreak risk and guide policy adjustments to reduce transmission windows in high-risk groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The model was not designed to directly compare alternative vaccination schedules, and external comparisons were limited by varying data quality and outbreak contexts. The evidence strength is moderate, as the approach is biologically plausible and validated against observed outbreaks, but relies on assumptions about vaccine effectiveness and assay accuracy.

应对 麻疹 流行病:学校可以用来促进童年的循证方法 麻疹。

Addressing the Measles Epidemic: Evidence Informed Approaches Schools Can Use to Promote Childhood Vaccination.
作者： Brian N Fink, Fink BN., Brian N. Fink, Brian Fink
摘要中文翻译作者声明不存在利益冲突。数据共享不适用于本文,因为当前研究期间没有生成或分析数据集。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 麻疹 在美国重新出现的流行病威胁,尽管该病已于 2000 年消除,并探讨了在 麻疹 发病率下降和政策变化的情况下学校在促进儿童 麻疹 方面的作用。
设计与对象
该研究是一项叙述性回顾,重点关注 2025 年 1 月至 2026 年 4 月 麻疹 在美国的复苏,特别关注学龄儿童以及联邦政策转变的影响。
核心方法
该分析基于对报告的 麻疹 案件、联邦法院判决和政策变化的审查,而不生成或分析新的数据集。
主要结果
2025年1月至2026年4月,美国确诊了4102例麻疹病例,其中92.5%的病例未接种疫苗或疫苗接种状态未知;23.7%的病例为5岁以下儿童,47.4%为5-19岁儿童。
解释与创新
作者将疫情死灰复燃的原因解释为 疫苗接种 比率下降、免疫政策政治化以及机构权威受到侵蚀,而联邦政策的转变加剧了疫情。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,学校需要实施以证据为依据的策略,以促进 疫苗接种、保护弱势学生、管理 暴发 并反击两极分化环境中的错误信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于报告的案例和政策分析,没有主要数据收集或对照实验,并且叙述性审查方法可能会引入偏差。

Addressing the Measles Epidemic: Evidence Informed Approaches Schools Can Use to Promote Childhood Vaccination.

Authors: Brian N Fink, Fink BN., Brian N. Fink, Brian Fink
Original abstract (English)
The author declares no conflicts of interest. Data sharing not applicable to this article as no datasets were generated or analysed during the current study.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the re-emergence of measles as an epidemic threat in the United States, despite its elimination in 2000, and examines the role of schools in promoting childhood vaccination amid declining vaccination rates and policy changes.
Design and population
The study is a narrative review focusing on the resurgence of measles in the United States from January 2025 to April 2026, with a particular emphasis on school-aged children and the impact of federal policy shifts.
Core methods
The analysis is based on a review of reported measles cases, federal court decisions, and policy changes, without generating or analyzing new datasets.
Main results
From January 2025 to April 2026, there were 4,102 diagnosed measles cases in the United States, with 92.5% of cases being unvaccinated or having unknown vaccination status; 23.7% of cases were in children under 5 years, and 47.4% in those aged 5–19.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the resurgence as a consequence of declining vaccination rates, politicization of immunization policy, and erosion of institutional authority, with federal policy shifts exacerbating the epidemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for schools to implement evidence-informed strategies to promote vaccination, protect vulnerable students, manage outbreaks, and counter misinformation in polarized environments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on reported cases and policy analyses without primary data collection or controlled experiments, and the narrative review approach may introduce bias.

胡志明市流行病学和传播动态的变化:2018-2020年和2024-2025年暴发的比较

Changing epidemiology and transmission dynamics of measles in Ho Chi Minh City: a comparison of the 2018–2020 and 2024–2025 outbreaks
作者： Lan Truong Thi Thanh, Anh Huynh, Khang Huynh, Kieu Diem Le, Thu Pham, Tran, A., Tuyen Pham Thi Kim, T. T. Thanh Nguyen, Nga Le Hong, Tam Nguyen Hong
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 麻疹 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生威胁。随着病例再次出现,胡志明市(HCMC)在2024-2025年经历了与2018-2020年疫情不同的严重暴发。我们比较了这两个 暴发 的 流行病学 和 传播 动态,以告知未来的 监测 和 疫苗接种 策略。方法从传染病麻疹系统中提取2018年至2025年胡志明市报告的麻疹病例的列表数据。使用横截面年龄分布和纵向出生 队列研究 分析来评估人口变化。传播 动力学的特征是使用 Cori 和 Wallinga-Teunis 方法估计有效繁殖数 (Rt)。构建了按年龄分层的感染者 传播 矩阵,以量化干预前后年龄组内和年龄组之间的 传播。结果 2018-2020 年 暴发 主要影响 5 岁以下儿童(中位年龄 3.75 岁,IQR 0.9-9.1),而 2024-2025 年 暴发 则转向较大年龄组(中位年龄 8.4 岁,IQR 1-14)。Birth-队列研究 分析确定了两个主要的免疫缺口:在 COVID-19 免疫接种中断期间(2019-2023 年)出生的儿童以及 2014 年之前出生的不符合 2018 年 暴发 反应免疫接种活动资格的青少年和年轻人。与早期疫情相比,2024-2025 年 暴发 还表现出更广泛的空间传播和逆转的地理轨迹。传播模式发生了很大变化,2018-2020年的传播主要由幼儿中的传播维持,而2024-2025年的传播则由较大年龄组驱动,其中>15岁的个体是婴儿的重要感染源。结论 HCMC 中 麻疹 的 流行病学 已从以儿童为主的 传播 转向青少年和成人的参与增加,反映了多胎 队列研究 中累积的免疫差距。这些发现表明,年龄限制的 暴发 反应免疫可能会留下残留的易感人群,从而为未来的 暴发 做出贡献。要实现可持续的 麻疹 消除,除了维持儿童 麻疹 的高常规覆盖率外,还需要 疫苗接种 策略来解决所有弱势年龄组的历史免疫差距。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生威胁。本研究旨在了解胡志明市 麻疹 的 流行病学 和 传播 动态变化。
设计与对象
该研究比较了 2018-2020 年和 2024-2025 年胡志明市的 麻疹 暴发。麻疹 病例的名单数据是从 2018 年至 2025 年传染病 监测 系统中提取的。
核心方法
使用横截面年龄分布和纵向出生 队列研究 分析来评估人口变化。传播 动力学的特征是使用 Cori 和 Wallinga-Teunis 方法估计有效繁殖数 (Rt)。
主要结果
2018-2020年暴发主要影响五岁以下儿童(中位年龄3.75岁,IQR 0.9-9.1),而2024-2025年暴发则转向较大年龄组(中位年龄8.4岁,IQR 1-14)。与早期疫情相比,2024-2025 年 暴发 还表现出更广泛的空间传播和逆转的地理轨迹。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,年龄限制的 暴发 反应免疫可能会留下残留的易感人群,从而为未来的 暴发 做出贡献。传播模式发生了很大变化,2018-2020年的传播主要由幼儿中的传播维持,而2024-2025年的传播则由较大年龄组驱动,其中15岁以上的个体是婴儿的重要感染源。
科研与公卫意义
要实现可持续的 麻疹 消除,除了维持儿童 麻疹 的高常规覆盖率外,还需要 疫苗接种 策略来解决所有弱势年龄组的历史免疫差距。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Changing epidemiology and transmission dynamics of measles in Ho Chi Minh City: a comparison of the 2018–2020 and 2024–2025 outbreaks

Authors: Lan Truong Thi Thanh, Anh Huynh, Khang Huynh, Kieu Diem Le, Thu Pham, Tran, A., Tuyen Pham Thi Kim, T. T. Thanh Nguyen, Nga Le Hong, Tam Nguyen Hong
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Measles remains a major public health threat worldwide. Following a resurgence of cases, Ho Chi Minh City (HCMC) experienced a severe outbreak in 2024–2025 that differed from the 2018–2020 epidemic. We compared the epidemiology and transmission dynamics of these two outbreaks to inform future surveillance and vaccination strategies. Methods Line-list data for measles cases reported in HCMC between 2018 and 2025 were extracted from the infectious disease surveillance system. Demographic changes were assessed using cross-sectional age distributions and longitudinal birth-cohort analyses. Transmission dynamics were characterized by estimating the effective reproduction number (Rt) using the Cori and Wallinga– Teunis methods. Age-stratified Who-Infected-Whom transmission matrices were constructed to quantify within- and between-age-group transmission before and after interventions. Results The 2018–2020 outbreak primarily affected children under five years of age (median age 3.75 years, IQR 0.9–9.1), whereas the 2024–2025 outbreak shifted toward older age groups (median age 8.4 years, IQR 1–14). Birth-cohort analyses identified two major immunity gaps: children born during the COVID-19 immunization disruption (2019–2023) and adolescents and young adults born before 2014 who were not eligible for the 2018 outbreak response immunization campaign. The 2024–2025 outbreak also showed broader spatial spread and a reversed geographic trajectory compared with the earlier epidemic. Transmission patterns changed substantially, with the 2018– 2020 outbreak sustained mainly by transmission among young children, whereas the 2024–2025 outbreak was driven by older age groups, with individuals >15 years acting as an important source of infection for infants. Conclusion The epidemiology of measles in HCMC has shifted from predominantly pediatric transmission to increasing involvement of adolescents and adults, reflecting accumulated immunity gaps across multiple birth cohorts. These findings suggest that age-restricted outbreak response immunization may leave residual susceptible populations that contribute to future outbreaks. Achieving sustainable measles elimination will require vaccination strategies that address historical immunity gaps across all vulnerable age groups, in addition to maintaining high routine childhood vaccination coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a major public health threat worldwide. The study aimed to understand the changing epidemiology and transmission dynamics of measles in Ho Chi Minh City.
Design and population
The study compared measles outbreaks in Ho Chi Minh City during 2018–2020 and 2024–2025. Line-list data for measles cases were extracted from the infectious disease surveillance system between 2018 and 2025.
Core methods
Demographic changes were assessed using cross-sectional age distributions and longitudinal birth-cohort analyses. Transmission dynamics were characterized by estimating the effective reproduction number (Rt) using the Cori and Wallinga–Teunis methods.
Main results
The 2018–2020 outbreak primarily affected children under five years of age (median age 3.75 years, IQR 0.9–9.1), whereas the 2024–2025 outbreak shifted toward older age groups (median age 8.4 years, IQR 1–14). The 2024–2025 outbreak also showed broader spatial spread and a reversed geographic trajectory compared with the earlier epidemic.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that age-restricted outbreak response immunization may leave residual susceptible populations that contribute to future outbreaks. Transmission patterns changed substantially, with the 2018–2020 outbreak sustained mainly by transmission among young children, whereas the 2024–2025 outbreak was driven by older age groups, with individuals >15 years acting as an important source of infection for infants.
Scientific and public-health significance
Achieving sustainable measles elimination will require vaccination strategies that address historical immunity gaps across all vulnerable age groups, in addition to maintaining high routine childhood vaccination coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

麻疹 控制的风险针对性干预措施——刚果民主共和国加丹加地区应急响应项目的经验教训(2021-2023 年)

Risk-targeted interventions for measles control-Lessons from an emergency response project in the Katanga Region, Democratic Republic of the Congo (2021-2023)
作者： Birgit Nikolay, Halidou Salou, Charles Matungulu, Gisèle Mbuyi, Noella Mosala, Dominique Muteba, Catherine Eisenhauer, Jean Marie Kafwembe, Patrick Banza Mpiong, Francis Kambol
摘要中文翻译摘要 Urgepi 项目是无国界医生组织巴黎行动中心 (MSF-OCP) 在刚果民主共和国加丹加地区实施的一项 麻疹 紧急举措,该地区经常出现 麻疹 流行病,对公共卫生造成重大影响。为了最大限度地发挥影响,该项目采用以风险为目标的方法,将资源引导到选定的卫生区,以加强监测,实施预防性疫苗接种,并确保及时应对疫情。本研究提出了Urgepi方法,并基于2021-2023年麻疹数据,评估有针对性的预防活动的有效性,探索在多个警报中优先干预的策略,并评估干预措施对麻疹流行病的总体影响。预防性疫苗接种在9个接种疫苗的高风险卫生区中的6个成功避免了流行病,而10个未接种疫苗的重点卫生区中的9个出现了大规模流行病。在经历过流行病的三个疫苗接种卫生区,需要采取更适合当地的 疫苗接种 策略才能实现更高的覆盖率。关于该项目确定调查和干预警报优先级的方法,我们发现 Urgepi 警报优先级评分与随后的流行病规模呈正相关,这表明这种方法可以有效、及时地针对较大的当地 麻疹 流行病。尽管没有观察到流行病应对 疫苗接种 对病例数的明显影响,可能是由于通过提供免费病例管理在病例报告中引入了偏差,但流行病持续时间中位数减少了 50%。总之,研究结果证明了 Urgepi 项目针对 麻疹 预防和控制的风险针对性方法的有效性,并强调了及时和针对具体情况的干预措施的重要性。关键问题 关于该主题的已知信息 – 理论上,可以通过预防性 麻疹 或 麻疹 期间的快速免疫反应来有效控制 麻疹。然而,许多国家继续面临重大的运营挑战。这项研究的补充内容——我们展示了以风险为目标的方法,并优先考虑预防和响应行动,可以在对抗 麻疹 的斗争中优化资源。这项研究可能如何影响研究、实践或政策——当由于操作限制而无法覆盖所有地区时,类似的风险目标方法可以帮助国家计划优先考虑 麻疹 预防和 麻疹 应对的地理区域。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决刚果民主共和国加丹加地区 麻疹 控制的操作挑战,该地区反复流行的流行病对公共卫生产生重大影响,其目的是评估 麻疹 预防和控制的风险针对性方法的有效性。
设计与对象
该研究于2021年至2023年在刚果民主共和国加丹加地区进行,重点关注选定的高风险卫生区,并利用监测数据评估了Urgepi项目的风险针对性方法。
核心方法
该研究评估了有针对性的预防活动,探索了在多个警报中优先考虑干预措施的策略,并使用 2021-2023 年 麻疹 数据评估了干预措施对 麻疹 流行病的影响。
主要结果
预防性 疫苗接种 在 9 个已接种疫苗的高风险卫生区中的 6 个避免了流行病,而 10 个未接种疫苗的重点卫生区中有 9 个出现了大规模流行病;Urgepi 警报优先级评分与随后的流行病规模呈正相关;流行病持续时间中位数减少了 50%。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明了针对 麻疹 预防和控制的风险针对性方法的有效性,强调了及时和针对具体情况的干预措施的重要性,并证明了这种方法如何在对抗 麻疹 的过程中优化资源。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为国家计划提供信息,说明当操作限制无法覆盖所有地区时,如何优先考虑 麻疹 预防和 麻疹 响应的地理区域,并证明以风险为目标的方法在优化资源分配方面的价值。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为没有观察到流行病应对 疫苗接种 对病例数的明显影响,这可能是由于通过提供免费病例管理在病例报告中引入了偏差,并且该研究的有效性仅限于加丹加地区的具体情况。

Risk-targeted interventions for measles control-Lessons from an emergency response project in the Katanga Region, Democratic Republic of the Congo (2021-2023)

Authors: Birgit Nikolay, Halidou Salou, Charles Matungulu, Gisèle Mbuyi, Noella Mosala, Dominique Muteba, Catherine Eisenhauer, Jean Marie Kafwembe, Patrick Banza Mpiong, Francis Kambol
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The Urgepi project is a measles emergency initiative implemented by Médecins Sans Frontières Operational Centre Paris (MSF-OCP) in the Katanga region of the Democratic Republic of Congo, a setting characterized by recurrent measles epidemics with substantial public health impact. To maximize impact, the project employs a risk-targeted approach directing resources to selected health zones to strengthen surveillance, implement preventive vaccination, and ensure timely epidemic response. This study presents the Urgepi approach, and, based on surveillance data from 2021-2023, evaluates the effectiveness of targeted preventive activities, explores strategies for prioritizing interventions amid multiple alerts, and assesses the overall impact of interventions on measles epidemics. Preventive vaccination successfully averted epidemics in six of the nine vaccinated high-risk health zones, while nine of ten non-vaccinated priority health zones experienced large epidemics. In the three vaccinated health zones that experienced epidemics, more locally adapted vaccination strategies would have been required to achieve higher coverage. Regarding the project’s approach to prioritize alerts for investigations and interventions, we found that the Urgepi alert prioritization score was positively associated with subsequent epidemic size, suggesting that this approach allowed effective and timely targeting of larger local measles epidemics. Although no clear impact of epidemic response vaccination on case numbers was observed, likely due to biases introduced in case reporting through provision of free case management, a 50% reduction in the median epidemic duration was recorded. In conclusion, the findings demonstrate the effectiveness of the Urgepi project’s risk-targeted approach for measles prevention and control and highlight the importance of timely and context-specific interventions. Key questions What is already known on this topic – Measles can, in theory, be effectively controlled through preventive vaccination or rapid immunization responses during outbreaks. However, many countries continue to face significant operational challenges. What this study adds – We demonstrate how a risk-targeted approach, with prioritization of both preventive and response actions, can optimize resources in the fight against measles. How this study might affect research, practice or policy – Similar risk-targeted approaches can help national programs prioritize geographic areas for measles prevention and outbreak response when it is not feasible to cover all regions due to operational constraints.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address operational challenges in measles control in the Katanga region of the Democratic Republic of Congo, where recurrent epidemics have substantial public health impact, and it aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of a risk-targeted approach for measles prevention and control.
Design and population
The study was conducted in the Katanga region of the Democratic Republic of Congo from 2021 to 2023, focusing on selected high-risk health zones, and evaluated the Urgepi project's risk-targeted approach using surveillance data.
Core methods
The study evaluated targeted preventive activities, explored strategies for prioritizing interventions amid multiple alerts, and assessed the impact of interventions on measles epidemics using surveillance data from 2021-2023.
Main results
Preventive vaccination averted epidemics in six of nine vaccinated high-risk health zones, while nine of ten non-vaccinated priority health zones experienced large epidemics; the Urgepi alert prioritization score was positively associated with subsequent epidemic size; and a 50% reduction in median epidemic duration was recorded.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of the risk-targeted approach for measles prevention and control, highlighting the importance of timely and context-specific interventions, and demonstrated how such an approach can optimize resources in the fight against measles.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform national programs on how to prioritize geographic areas for measles prevention and outbreak response when operational constraints make it infeasible to cover all regions, and demonstrate the value of risk-targeted approaches in optimizing resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as no clear impact of epidemic response vaccination on case numbers was observed, likely due to biases introduced in case reporting through provision of free case management, and the study's effectiveness is limited to the specific context of the Katanga region.

调查美国的 麻疹 案件、疫苗接种 比率和在家上学的三重奏

Investigating the Trifecta of Measles Cases, Vaccination Rates, and Homeschooling in the United States
作者： Virginia C. Hughes
摘要中文翻译尽管存在耐受性良好且有效的 疫苗,但过去几年美国 麻疹 病例的死灰复燃引发了民众对住院率和 死亡率 率增加的担忧。麻疹 是一种高度传染性的病毒性疾病,通过气溶胶和呼吸道飞沫在空气中传播,主要影响年轻人。进行了一项研究,采用统计检验多元回归和皮尔逊相关系数来评估在家上学和 麻疹 率对 麻疹 案例的影响。自变量包括 2015 年至 2025 年 MMR 平均 麻疹 率和 2024 年在家上学数据。回归分析显示,在家上学对 麻疹 病例数有显着影响 (P=.004),且呈正相关 (r=0.4,P=0.01)。疫苗接种 速率变量未发现统计显着性,但相关系数为正(r=0.06,P>0.05)。随着研究人员继续探索美国 麻疹 病例近期激增的原因,这些发现丰富了当前的知识体系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究是必要的,因为美国 麻疹 病例再次出现,这引发了人们对住院率和 死亡率 率增加的担忧,尽管有一种耐受性良好且有效的 疫苗。
设计与对象
一项研究旨在评估美国在家上学和 麻疹 比率对 麻疹 病例的影响。
核心方法
该研究采用统计检验、多元回归和皮尔逊相关系数来分析2015年至2025年MMR平均麻疹率、2024年在家教育数据和麻疹病例之间的关系。
主要结果
回归分析显示,在家上学对麻疹病例数有显着影响(P=.004),且呈正相关(r=0.4,P=0.01),而疫苗接种率变量虽然相关系数为正,但不具有统计学意义(r=0.06,P>0.05)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为对当前知识体系的补充,研究人员继续探索美国 麻疹 病例近期激增的原因。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在家上学可能是导致 麻疹 病例复发的一个因素,凸显了需要进一步调查 麻疹 疾病中的非 麻疹 相关因素。
局限与证据强度
证据强度为中等,因为该研究确定了在家上学和 麻疹 案例之间存在显着相关性,但没有确定因果关系,并且 疫苗接种 率变量不具有统计显着性。

Investigating the Trifecta of Measles Cases, Vaccination Rates, and Homeschooling in the United States

Authors: Virginia C. Hughes
Original abstract (English)
The resurgence in measles cases in the United States over the past couple of years has sparked concern among the populace in the sphere of increased hospitalizations and mortality rates despite the availability of a well tolerated and effective vaccine. Measles is a highly contagious viral disease which is spread through the air via aerosols and respiratory droplets and primarily affects younger individuals. A study was done to assess the effect of homeschooling and vaccination rates on Measles cases employing statistical tests multiple regression and Pearson correlation coefficient. Independent variables comprised MMR average vaccination rates from 2015 to 2025, and homeschooling data from 2024. Regression analysis revealed homeschooling had a significant effect on the number of measles cases (P=.004) with a positive correlation (r=0.4, P=0.01). The vaccination rate variable was not found to be statistically significant, however the correlation coefficient was positive (r=0.06, P>0.05). These findings add to the current body of knowledge as researchers continue to explore causes for the recent spike in measles cases in the United States.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed due to the resurgence in measles cases in the United States, which has sparked concern about increased hospitalizations and mortality rates despite the availability of a well-tolerated and effective vaccine.
Design and population
A study was conducted to assess the effect of homeschooling and vaccination rates on measles cases in the United States.
Core methods
The study employed statistical tests, multiple regression, and Pearson correlation coefficient to analyze the relationship between MMR average vaccination rates from 2015 to 2025, homeschooling data from 2024, and measles cases.
Main results
Regression analysis revealed homeschooling had a significant effect on the number of measles cases (P=.004) with a positive correlation (r=0.4, P=0.01), while the vaccination rate variable was not statistically significant, though the correlation coefficient was positive (r=0.06, P>0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as adding to the current body of knowledge as researchers continue to explore causes for the recent spike in measles cases in the United States.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that homeschooling may be a contributing factor to the resurgence of measles cases, highlighting the need for further investigation into non-vaccination-related factors in disease transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study identifies a significant correlation between homeschooling and measles cases but does not establish causation, and the vaccination rate variable was not statistically significant.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2023-2024 年世卫组织东地中海区域 麻疹 和风疹消除趋势:进展和近期流行病学变化

Measles and Rubella Elimination Trends in the WHO Eastern Mediterranean Region, 2023–2024: Progress and Recent Epidemiological Shifts
作者： Muhammad Farid, Mohammad Nadir Sahak, Isaac Baffoe-Nyarko, Amany Ghoniem, Sharifuzamman Sharifuzamman, Eman Abd Elmahdy, Quamrul Hasan, Mohammed Osama Mere, Palwasha Anwari, Osama Mere
摘要中文翻译背景:麻疹 和风疹仍然是免疫系统绩效的关键指标,也是 2030 年免疫议程 (IA2030) 的核心。COVID-19 大流行造成的破坏扩大了免疫差距,并加速了 疫苗 可预防疾病在多个地区的死灰复燃。本研究评估了 2023 年至 2024 年世卫组织东地中海区域 (EMR) 在 麻疹 和消除风疹方面取得的进展。方法:我们对所有 22 个 EMR 国家和地区的 麻疹 和风疹 监测 数据、世界卫生组织/联合国儿童基金会国家免疫覆盖率估算 (WUENIC) 以及联合报告表 (eJRF) 数据进行了回顾性描述性分析。关键指标包括疾病 发病率、MCV1 和 MCV2 覆盖率、监测 绩效指标以及经过验证的消除状态。结果:区域性 麻疹 发病率 从每百万人口 110.8 增加到 116.3,而风疹 发病率 从每百万人口 1.9 增加到 4.3,增加了一倍多。也门、伊拉克和阿富汗的大型 暴发 不成比例地推动了区域趋势。MCV1 覆盖率稳定在 80% 左右,而 MCV2 覆盖率从 73% 略有上升至 75%。到 2024 年,22 个国家中只有 9 个国家的两种剂量覆盖率达到 ≥95%。持续的零剂量人群、高退出率和持续的免疫差距在多种环境下维持了 传播。结论:EMR 仍无法实现到 2030 年实现 麻疹 和消除风疹的目标。迫切需要加速、注重公平的努力,以加强常规免疫系统、减少零剂量人群、增强 麻疹 能力并改善 麻疹 应对——特别是在脆弱和受冲突影响的环境中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 和风疹仍然是免疫系统绩效的关键指标,也是 2030 年免疫议程 (IA2030) 的核心。COVID-19 大流行造成的破坏扩大了免疫差距,并加速了 疫苗 可预防疾病在多个地区的死灰复燃。
设计与对象
本研究评估了 2023 年至 2024 年世卫组织东地中海区域 (EMR) 在 麻疹 和消除风疹方面取得的进展。关键指标包括疾病 发病率、MCV1 和 MCV2 覆盖率、监测 绩效指标以及经过验证的消除状态。
核心方法
我们对所有 22 个 EMR 国家和地区的 麻疹 和风疹 监测 数据、世界卫生组织/联合国儿童基金会国家免疫覆盖率估算 (WUENIC) 以及联合报告表 (eJRF) 数据进行了回顾性描述性分析。
主要结果
区域性 麻疹 发病率 从每百万人口 110.8 增加到 116.3,而风疹 发病率 从每百万人口 1.9 增加到 4.3,增加了一倍多。也门、伊拉克和阿富汗的大型 暴发 不成比例地推动了区域趋势。MCV1 覆盖率稳定在 80% 左右,而 MCV2 覆盖率从 73% 略有上升至 75%。到 2024 年,22 个国家中只有 9 个国家的两种剂量覆盖率达到 ≥95%。持续的零剂量人群、高退出率和持续的免疫差距在多种环境下维持了 传播。
解释与创新
EMR 仍无法实现到 2030 年实现 麻疹 和消除风疹的目标。迫切需要加速、注重公平的努力,以加强常规免疫系统、减少零剂量人群、增强 麻疹 能力并改善 监测 应对——特别是在脆弱和受冲突影响的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决免疫差距并加强电子病历中的免疫系统,特别是在经历 暴发 和低 疫苗接种 覆盖率的国家。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,并未考虑免疫实践或 暴发 动态的实时变化。由于依赖于报告的数据而不是直接观察或实验验证,证据的强度中等。

Measles and Rubella Elimination Trends in the WHO Eastern Mediterranean Region, 2023–2024: Progress and Recent Epidemiological Shifts

Authors: Muhammad Farid, Mohammad Nadir Sahak, Isaac Baffoe-Nyarko, Amany Ghoniem, Sharifuzamman Sharifuzamman, Eman Abd Elmahdy, Quamrul Hasan, Mohammed Osama Mere, Palwasha Anwari, Osama Mere
Original abstract (English)
Background: Measles and rubella remain key indicators of immunization system performance and are central to the Immunization Agenda 2030 (IA2030). Disruptions caused by the COVID-19 pandemic have widened immunity gaps and accelerated resurgence of vaccine-preventable diseases across multiple regions. This study assesses progress toward measles and rubella elimination in the WHO Eastern Mediterranean Region (EMR) during 2023–2024. Methods: We conducted a retrospective descriptive analysis of measles and rubella surveillance data, WHO/UNICEF Estimates of National Immunization Coverage (WUENIC), and Joint Reporting Form (eJRF) data for all 22 EMR countries and territories. Key indicators included disease incidence, MCV1 and MCV2 coverage, surveillance performance metrics, and verified elimination status. Results: Regional measles incidence increased from 110.8 to 116.3 per million population, while rubella incidence more than doubled from 1.9 to 4.3 per million. Large outbreaks in Yemen, Iraq, and Afghanistan disproportionally drove regional trends. MCV1 coverage remained stable at approximately 80%, while MCV2 coverage increased marginally from 73% to 75%. Only nine of 22 countries achieved ≥95% coverage for both doses in 2024. Persistent zero-dose populations, high dropout rates, and ongoing immunity gaps sustained transmission across multiple settings. Conclusions: The EMR remains off track to achieve measles and rubella elimination by 2030. Accelerated, equity-focused efforts are urgently needed to strengthen routine immunization systems, reduce zero-dose population, enhance surveillance capacity, and improve outbreak response—particularly in fragile and conflicted-affected settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles and rubella remain key indicators of immunization system performance and are central to the Immunization Agenda 2030 (IA2030). Disruptions caused by the COVID-19 pandemic have widened immunity gaps and accelerated resurgence of vaccine-preventable diseases across multiple regions.
Design and population
This study assesses progress toward measles and rubella elimination in the WHO Eastern Mediterranean Region (EMR) during 2023–2024. Key indicators included disease incidence, MCV1 and MCV2 coverage, surveillance performance metrics, and verified elimination status.
Core methods
We conducted a retrospective descriptive analysis of measles and rubella surveillance data, WHO/UNICEF Estimates of National Immunization Coverage (WUENIC), and Joint Reporting Form (eJRF) data for all 22 EMR countries and territories.
Main results
Regional measles incidence increased from 110.8 to 116.3 per million population, while rubella incidence more than doubled from 1.9 to 4.3 per million. Large outbreaks in Yemen, Iraq, and Afghanistan disproportionally drove regional trends. MCV1 coverage remained stable at approximately 80%, while MCV2 coverage increased marginally from 73% to 75%. Only nine of 22 countries achieved ≥95% coverage for both doses in 2024. Persistent zero-dose populations, high dropout rates, and ongoing immunity gaps sustained transmission across multiple settings.
Interpretation and novelty
The EMR remains off track to achieve measles and rubella elimination by 2030. Accelerated, equity-focused efforts are urgently needed to strengthen routine immunization systems, reduce zero-dose population, enhance surveillance capacity, and improve outbreak response—particularly in fragile and conflicted-affected settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to address immunity gaps and strengthen immunization systems in the EMR, particularly in countries experiencing outbreaks and low vaccination coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data and does not account for real-time changes in immunization practices or outbreak dynamics. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on reported data rather than direct observation or experimental validation.

巴基斯坦 麻疹 暴发 的调查(2022 年至 2023 年):探索合并症、并发症和分子特征

Investigation of measles outbreak in Pakistan (2022 to 2023): Exploring comorbidities, complications, and molecular characterization
作者： Asia Nawaz, Sidra Rahman, Nighat Haider, Adnan Zeb, Muhammad Ali
摘要中文翻译背景 麻疹 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖和医疗基础设施不足的地区,例如巴基斯坦。本研究的目的是调查 2022 年至 2023 年巴基斯坦 麻疹 暴发 的流行病学、分子和系统发育特征,并强调有助于更好地了解该疾病的改进领域,并为有效的 麻疹 管理和 疫苗接种 优化提供策略。方法 收集2022年12月至2023年12月巴基斯坦伊斯兰堡PIMS收治的患者(n = 183)的咽拭子。使用SPSS-21软件对数据进行统计分析。利用不同的生物信息学软件进行系统发育、氨基酸取代及其功能分析以及部分核蛋白的结构分析。结果 大多数 麻疹 患者是具有较高 患病率 合并症和并发症的婴儿,并且大量患者的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围不足。系统发育分析揭示了与俄罗斯和美国流行的 B3 菌株的相关性,强调了全球传播。氨基酸取代和结构分析强调了当前分离株与参考菌株相比的微小结构变化。结论 研究结果强调了持续 麻疹 死亡率 和了解 麻疹 流行病学 和病毒变异对于有效 暴发 管理和 疫苗接种 优化的重要性,最终旨在减轻巴基斯坦和全球类似环境中与 麻疹 死亡率 相关的发病率和 死亡率。这项研究例证了跨学科合作在应对复杂传染病挑战方面的重要性,强调了通过 麻疹 对抗 麻疹 进行持续监测和准备的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖和医疗基础设施不足的地区,例如巴基斯坦。
设计与对象
本研究的目的是调查 2022 年至 2023 年巴基斯坦 麻疹 暴发 的流行病学、分子和系统发育特征,并强调有助于更好地了解该疾病的改进领域,并为有效的 麻疹 管理和 疫苗接种 优化提供策略。
核心方法
咽拭子采集自 2022 年 12 月至 2023 年 12 月在巴基斯坦伊斯兰堡 PIMS 收治的患者 (n = 183)。使用 SPSS-21 软件对数据进行统计分析。
主要结果
大多数 麻疹 患者是 患病率 合并症和并发症较高的婴儿,并且大量患者的 疫苗接种 覆盖范围不足。系统发育分析揭示了与俄罗斯和美国流行的 B3 菌株的相关性,强调了全球传播。氨基酸取代和结构分析强调了当前分离株与参考菌株相比的微小结构变化。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了持续 监测 以及理解 麻疹、流行病学 和病毒变异对于有效的 暴发 管理和 疫苗接种 优化的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进 疫苗接种 策略和 监测 系统,以减轻巴基斯坦和全球类似环境中与 麻疹 死亡率 相关的发病率和 死亡率。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Investigation of measles outbreak in Pakistan (2022 to 2023): Exploring comorbidities, complications, and molecular characterization

Authors: Asia Nawaz, Sidra Rahman, Nighat Haider, Adnan Zeb, Muhammad Ali
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Measles remains a major public health concern worldwide, particularly in regions with insufficient vaccination coverage and healthcare infrastructure, like Pakistan. The purpose of this study is to investigate the epidemiological, molecular, and phylogenetic characteristics of the measles outbreak in Pakistan during 2022-2023 and highlight the areas of improvement that can contribute to the better understanding of the disease and inform strategies for effective outbreak management and vaccination optimization. METHODS Throat swabs were collected from patients (n = 183) admitted at PIMS, Islamabad, Pakistan, from December 2022- December 2023. Statistical analysis of data was conducted on SPSS-21 software. The phylogenetics, amino acid substitutions, their functional analysis, and structural analysis of partial nucleoprotein were performed by using different bioinformatics software. RESULTS Most of the measles patients were infants with a high prevalence of comorbidity and complications, and significant number of patients had inadequate vaccination coverage. The phylogenetic analysis revealed relatedness to B3 strains circulating in Russia and the USA, emphasizing the global spread. The amino acid substitutions and structure analysis highlighted minor structural variations in current isolates as compared to the reference strain. CONCLUSION The findings underscore the importance of continuous surveillance and understanding of measles epidemiology and viral variations for effective outbreak management and vaccination optimization, ultimately aiming to mitigate the morbidity and mortality associated with measles outbreaks in Pakistan and similar settings globally. This study exemplifies the significance of interdisciplinary collaboration in confronting complex infectious disease challenges, emphasizing the imperative of continuous monitoring and preparedness in combating measles through vaccination.

The phylogenetic analysis revealed relatedness to B3 strains circulating in Russia and the USA, emphasizing the global spread. The findings highlight the need for improved vaccination strategies and surveillance systems to mitigate the morbidity and mortality associated with measles outbreaks in Pakistan and similar settings globally.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

具有 麻疹 样症状的儿童(0-12 岁)中 麻疹 特异性 IgM 和 IgG 抗体的 血清流行率:尼日利亚东北部医院的横断面研究

Seroprevalence of measles-specific IgM and IgG antibodies among children (0–12 years) with measles-like symptoms: a hospital-based cross-sectional study in Northeast Nigeria
作者： Umar Abdullahi, Maryam Aminu, Ahmed Babangida Suleiman, Grace Sabo Nok Kia, Nnaemeka Darlington Ndodo, Olusola Anuoluwapo Akanbi, Chimaobi Chukwu, Khadijah Isa Imam, Richard Ojedele
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尼日利亚是全球 麻疹 负担最高和 麻疹 覆盖率最低的国家之一,尽管努力到 2030 年消除 麻疹,但由于冲突和 COVID-19 大流行,麻疹 仍然存在。
设计与对象
2022 年 11 月至 2023 年 7 月期间,从约贝、博尔诺和阿达马瓦选定的初级保健中心招募临床疑似 麻疹 病例的儿童,对血清样本进行 麻疹 特异性 IgM (n=558) 和 IgG (n=372) 抗体分析。
核心方法
使用 EUROIMMUN 麻疹病毒 IgM ELISA 试剂盒测定 IgM 抗体,使用 Calbiotech 麻疹 IgG 试剂盒测定 IgG 抗体,并使用单变量逻辑回归计算优势比和 95% 置信区间。
主要结果
The seroprevalence was 29.2% for measles IgM and 32.8% for measles IgG, with higher detection in males (30.6% and 33.3%) and children aged 1–3 years (40.8% and 46.5%), while vaccination significantly reduced infection odds (OR 0.30; 95% CI, 0.20–0.44; P ≤ 0.005).
解释与创新
该研究证实了尼日利亚东北部有症状儿童中 麻疹 血清阳性的发生率较高,这凸显了对有症状病例的免疫史进行局部调查的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了提高 疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 监测 的重要性,以减少受冲突影响且卫生基础设施中断的地区的 麻疹 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到潜在选择和回忆偏差的限制,其对有症状儿童的关注降低了普遍性,由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Seroprevalence of measles-specific IgM and IgG antibodies among children (0–12 years) with measles-like symptoms: a hospital-based cross-sectional study in Northeast Nigeria

Authors: Umar Abdullahi, Maryam Aminu, Ahmed Babangida Suleiman, Grace Sabo Nok Kia, Nnaemeka Darlington Ndodo, Olusola Anuoluwapo Akanbi, Chimaobi Chukwu, Khadijah Isa Imam, Richard Ojedele
Original abstract (English)
Nigeria has one of the highest measles burdens and the lowest vaccination coverage worldwide. The WHO AFRO region, including Nigeria, is currently working towards a revised target of eliminating measles by 2030. Nigeria’s strategy includes improving MCV1 coverage at 9 months, supplemental immunization for children aged 9–59 months, and intensified case-based surveillance. Recently, MCV2 was introduced and is being piloted. Achieving at least 95% coverage with MCV2 at 15 months is essential for herd immunity. Despite these efforts, measles outbreaks persist, worsened by armed conflicts and the COVID-19 pandemic in Northeast Nigeria which disrupts health infrastructures and ultimately affects immunization activities. This study evaluates serological patterns and confirms measles infection among symptomatic children attending healthcare facilities in Northeast Nigeria. By integrating serology with socio-demographic and clinical risk factors, we seek to evaluate the serological profile and identify proportion of susceptible individuals among symptomatic children in some selected clinical settings in Northeast Nigeria. Children with clinically suspected measles cases were recruited from selected primary healthcare centres (PHC) in Yobe, Borno and Adamawa between November 2022-July 2023. The study analysed serum samples for measles-specific IgM antibodies ( n = 558) and measles-specific IgG antibodies ( n = 372). IgM antibodies were determined using the EUROIMMUN measles virus IgM ELISA kit, and IgG antibodies were determined using the Calbiotech measles IgG kit. Odds Ratio (OR) and 95% Confidence Intervals (CI) were calculated from 2 × 2 tables using a Univariate Logistic Regression model. The association between measles and socio-demographic and risk factors was analysed using chi-square. Statistical significance was set at P ≤ 0.05. In this study, seroprevalence was defined by the presence of IgM to indicate recent infection and IgG to indicate past exposure or immunity. The results showed a seroprevalence of 29.2% for measles IgM and 32.8% for measles IgG. Measles IgM and IgG antibodies were detected more among males compared to females, with a seroprevalence of 30.6% and 33.3% respectively. No significant association was observed between sex and Measles virus seropositivity for either IgM or IgG ( P > 0.05). Although the odds ratio for males was 1.23, the 95% confidence interval (0.82–1.82) indicated that this difference was not statistically significant. The highest IgM and IgG antibodies were observed amongst children aged 1–3 years with a prevalence of 40.8% and 46.5% respectively. Vaccination was associated with a significant reduction in the odds of measles infection (OR 0.30; 95% CI, 0.20–0.44; P ≤ 0.005). This study is limited by potential selection and recall biases. By focusing exclusively on children presenting with measles-like symptoms, the findings are not representative of the general paediatric population. Furthermore, reliance on parental recall for vaccination history introduces a high likelihood of information bias. Our study identifies a high frequency of measles seropositivity among symptomatic children in selected healthcare facilities in Adamawa, Borno and Yobe states. While this study highlights a high prevalence of non-vaccination among symptomatic children, further research should focus on the need for localised investigation into immunization history in symptomatic cases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nigeria has one of the highest measles burdens and the lowest vaccination coverage worldwide, and despite efforts to eliminate measles by 2030, outbreaks persist due to conflicts and the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
Children with clinically suspected measles cases were recruited from selected primary healthcare centres in Yobe, Borno and Adamawa between November 2022-July 2023, with serum samples analysed for measles-specific IgM (n=558) and IgG (n=372) antibodies.
Core methods
IgM antibodies were determined using the EUROIMMUN measles virus IgM ELISA kit, and IgG antibodies were determined using the Calbiotech measles IgG kit, with odds ratios and 95% confidence intervals calculated using univariate logistic regression.
Main results
The seroprevalence was 29.2% for measles IgM and 32.8% for measles IgG, with higher detection in males (30.6% and 33.3%) and children aged 1–3 years (40.8% and 46.5%), while vaccination significantly reduced infection odds (OR 0.30; 95% CI, 0.20–0.44; P ≤ 0.005).
Interpretation and novelty
The study confirms a high frequency of measles seropositivity among symptomatic children in Northeast Nigeria, highlighting the need for localised investigation into immunization history in symptomatic cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of improving vaccination coverage and surveillance to reduce measles transmission in conflict-affected regions with disrupted health infrastructure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by potential selection and recall biases, and its focus on symptomatic children reduces generalisability, with evidence strength assessed as moderate due to these limitations.

2022 - 2024 年赞比亚南部省 麻疹 和急性弛缓性麻痹 监测 系统的评估

Evaluation of measles and acute flaccid paralysis surveillance systems in Southern Province, Zambia, 2022 - 2024
作者： Herbert Robert Mark Luhanga, Francis Ngosa Mwenya, Andrew Phiri, Patricia Simuzingili, Cheepa Habeenzu, James Exnobert Zulu, Jacob Sakala, Dabwitso Banda, Nyambe Sinyange
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:赞比亚的 疫苗-可预防疾病 (VPD) 监测 系统将常规免疫监测与疾病 监测 相结合,以指导控制措施。2022年,赞比亚记录了422例麻疹病例和37例死亡,其中南部省的病死率最高(11.2%),这表明监测的表现和暴发反应系统可能存在缺陷。本次评估评估了 2022 年至 2024 年赞比亚南部省的 麻疹 和急性弛缓性麻痹 (AFP) 监测 系统。方法:我们按照疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC) 指南进行了横断面 监测 系统评估。根据 麻疹 和 AFP 案件负担以及 疫苗接种 覆盖范围,我们有目的地选择了三个地区和每个地区的三个设施。我们使用结构化问卷从 27 名参与者那里收集了有关系统简单性、实用性和可接受性的数据。我们还从电子综合疾病 监测 和响应 (eIDSR) 系统和世界卫生组织数据库收集数据,以评估数据的完整性、及时性和准确性。使用频率和百分比总结结果。结果:麻疹 和 AFP 监测 系统包括在社区、卫生机构、地区和省级运行的被动和主动 麻疹 系统。这些系统表现出高度的简单性(平均分:84.6%)、实用性(90.5%)和可接受性(90.8%)。然而,监测 的性能受到数据质量次优的限制,平均完整性为 66.7%,及时性为 51.1%,数据准确性为 50%。我们还确定了标本运输不足和计划资金不足的关键运营挑战。结论:南方省的麻疹和AFP 监测系统功能齐全、简单、可接受,并且很好地融入日常服务。然而,数据质量的巨大差距限制了其早期 暴发 检测和及时公共卫生响应的有效性。加强数据管理、标本运输和可持续项目融资对于提高 监测 绩效和加快实现 疫苗 可预防疾病控制和消除目标至关重要。关键词:急性弛缓性麻痹 (AFP)、麻疹、监测 系统、疫苗 可预防疾病、赞比亚。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究评估了赞比亚南部省的 麻疹 和急性弛缓性麻痹 监测 系统,以评估其性能并确定数据质量和操作挑战方面的差距。
设计与对象
2022年至2024年,在赞比亚南部省进行了横断面的监测系统评估,根据麻疹和AFP案件负担以及疫苗接种覆盖率,有目的地选择三个地区和每个地区的三个设施。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷从 27 名参与者收集了有关系统简单性、有用性和可接受性的数据,同时从电子综合疾病 监测 和响应 (eIDSR) 系统和 WHO 数据库评估了数据完整性、及时性和准确性。
主要结果
监测 系统表现出高度的简单性 (84.6%)、有用性 (90.5%) 和可接受性 (90.8%),但数据质量欠佳,平均完整性为 66.7%、及时性为 51.1%、准确性为 50%,同时还存在样本运输不足和资金不足等操作挑战。
解释与创新
作者的结论是,虽然这些系统功能齐全且集成良好,但数据质量的显着差距限制了其早期 暴发 检测和及时公共卫生响应的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
加强数据管理、标本运输和可持续融资对于提高 监测 绩效和推进赞比亚 疫苗 可预防疾病控制和消除目标至关重要。
局限与证据强度
尽管评估遵循了 CDC 指南并包含多个数据源,但由于数据质量欠佳和操作挑战,证据强度中等。

Evaluation of measles and acute flaccid paralysis surveillance systems in Southern Province, Zambia, 2022 - 2024

Authors: Herbert Robert Mark Luhanga, Francis Ngosa Mwenya, Andrew Phiri, Patricia Simuzingili, Cheepa Habeenzu, James Exnobert Zulu, Jacob Sakala, Dabwitso Banda, Nyambe Sinyange
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: The Vaccine-Preventable Disease (VPD) surveillance system in Zambia integrates routine immunization monitoring with disease surveillance to guide control measures. In 2022, Zambia recorded 422 measles cases and 37 deaths, with Southern Province recording the highest case fatality rate (11.2%), suggesting possible weaknesses in surveillance performance and outbreak response systems. This evaluation assessed the measles and acute flaccid paralysis (AFP) surveillance systems in Southern province of Zambia from 2022 to 2024. Methods: We conducted a cross-sectional surveillance system evaluation following the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) guidelines. We purposively selected three districts and three facilities from each district, based on measles and AFP case burden and vaccination coverage. We collected data on system’s simplicity, usefulness, and acceptability from 27 participants using structured questionnaires. We also collected data from the electronic Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response (eIDSR) system and World Health Organization databases to assess, data completeness, timeliness, and accuracy. Results were summarized using frequencies and percentages. Results: The measles and AFP surveillance systems comprised both passive and active surveillance operating across community, health facility, district, and provincial levels. The systems demonstrated high levels of simplicity (mean score: 84.6%), usefulness (90.5%), and acceptability (90.8%). However, surveillance performance was limited by suboptimal data quality, with mean completeness of 66.7%, timeliness of 51.1%, and data accuracy of 50%. We also identified key operational challenges of inadequate specimen transportation and insufficient program funding. Conclusion: The measles and AFP surveillance systems in Southern Province are functional, simple, acceptable, and well-integrated into routine services. However, significant gaps in data quality limit their effectiveness for early outbreak detection and timely public health response. Strengthening data management, specimen transport, and sustainable program financing will be critical to improving surveillance performance and accelerating progress toward vaccine-preventable disease control and elimination targets. Keywords: Acute Flaccid Paralysis (AFP), measles, surveillance systems, vaccine-preventable disease, Zambia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study evaluated measles and acute flaccid paralysis surveillance systems in Southern Province, Zambia, to assess their performance and identify gaps in data quality and operational challenges.
Design and population
A cross-sectional surveillance system evaluation was conducted in Southern Province, Zambia, from 2022 to 2024, purposively selecting three districts and three facilities from each district based on measles and AFP case burden and vaccination coverage.
Core methods
Data on system simplicity, usefulness, and acceptability were collected from 27 participants using structured questionnaires, while data completeness, timeliness, and accuracy were assessed from the electronic Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response (eIDSR) system and WHO databases.
Main results
The surveillance systems demonstrated high simplicity (84.6%), usefulness (90.5%), and acceptability (90.8%), but suboptimal data quality with mean completeness of 66.7%, timeliness of 51.1%, and accuracy of 50%, alongside operational challenges like inadequate specimen transport and insufficient funding.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that while the systems are functional and well-integrated, significant gaps in data quality limit their effectiveness for early outbreak detection and timely public health response.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening data management, specimen transport, and sustainable financing is critical to improving surveillance performance and advancing vaccine-preventable disease control and elimination targets in Zambia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to suboptimal data quality and operational challenges, though the evaluation followed CDC guidelines and included multiple data sources.

乌干达婴儿 麻疹-风疹 疫苗 早期给药与标准给药时间的随机试验的初步安全性数据

Preliminary safety data from a randomised trial of early versus standard timing of administration of measles-rubella vaccine in Ugandan infants
作者： Gerald Businge, Natalie G. Marchevsky, Ezekiel Mupere, Sarah Kelly, Ilona Kakai, Mary Kyohere, Andrew Edielu, Fortunate Ambangira, Brenda Kakayi, Valerie Tusubira
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 麻疹 仍然是资源匮乏地区儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因。年幼的婴儿尤其面临严重疾病的风险,许多婴儿在六个月时缺乏保护性母体抗体水平。在高负担环境中九个月前使用 疫苗接种 可能会提供更早的保护;然而,人们对其在这个年龄段的有效性存在担忧。我们提出了有关注册、安全性和 麻疹 感染的早期研究结果,这些结果是在 6 个月龄与 9 个月龄(MR1)给予注册的含有 麻疹 的 麻疹 以及随后在 12 或 18 个月龄时进行加强注射(MR2)后的结果。方法 我们在乌干达坎帕拉的四个卫生机构进行了一项开放标签随机对照非劣效性试验。24-28 周的婴儿被随机分配在 6 个月和 12 个月(A 组)、9 个月和 18 个月(B 组)或 6 个月和 18 个月(C 组)时接受 MR。使用不同规模的分组随机化,按地点、母亲艾滋病毒状况和基线血红蛋白水平分层,以 1:1:1 的比例对婴儿进行电子随机化。看护人员将请求的反应记录在纸质日记卡上,并对安全相关事件进行评估。结果 450 名婴儿接受了 MR1 并被登记。在 6 个月和 9 个月的 MR1 组之间,未观察到 疫苗接种 后局部或全身事件的差异 [局部:21·2% 与 21·9% (p = 0·959),全身:29·2% 与 27·7% (p = 0·845)]。大多数局部反应都是轻微的,并且发生在最初的四天内。大多数 (5/6) 的住院治疗是因为常见的儿童疾病,其中 1 例与 疫苗 无关的死亡发生在 MR1 后六个月。登记了 16 例实验室确诊的 麻疹 病例(10 例)和风疹病例(6 例),以及 MR1 之前的 3 例临床 麻疹 病例。大多数 麻疹 病例 (70·0%) 发生在 C 组,MR2 后两周没有登记 麻疹 病例。结论 我们提供了关于在六个月大时给予含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 的安全性的进一步证据。试验注册:该试验于 2024 年 10 月 31 日在 ClinicalTrials.gov 注册,标识符为 NCT06667206。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是儿童发病的主要原因,而在资源匮乏的环境中,死亡率 和小婴儿尤其面临严重疾病的风险,许多婴儿在六个月时缺乏母体抗体的保护水平。
设计与对象
我们在乌干达坎帕拉的四个卫生机构进行了一项开放标签随机对照非劣效性试验,其中 24-28 周的婴儿被随机分配在 6 个月和 12 个月(A 组)、9 个月和 18 个月(B 组)或 6 个月和 18 个月(C 组)时接受 MR。
核心方法
使用不同大小的区块随机化,按地点、母亲艾滋病毒状况和基线血红蛋白水平分层,以 1:1:1 的比例对婴儿进行电子随机化,看护人员在纸质日记卡上记录征求的反应,并评估安全相关事件。
主要结果
450 名婴儿接受了 MR1 并入组;在 6 个月和 9 个月的 MR1 组之间,没有观察到 疫苗接种 后局部或全身事件的差异 [局部:21·2% 与 21·9% (p = 0·959),全身:29·2% 与 27·7% (p = 0·845)],并且大多数局部反应是轻微的,并且发生在前四天内。
解释与创新
我们提供了关于在六个月大时给予含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 的安全性的进一步证据,解决了对其在这个年龄的有效性的担忧。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持早期在高负担环境中使用 疫苗接种 可能会提供更早的保护,并且没有观察到安全风险增加。
局限与证据强度
由于研究结果的初步性质和开放标签设计,证据强度中等,尽管随机化和分层提高了可靠性,但可能会引入偏差。

Preliminary safety data from a randomised trial of early versus standard timing of administration of measles-rubella vaccine in Ugandan infants

Authors: Gerald Businge, Natalie G. Marchevsky, Ezekiel Mupere, Sarah Kelly, Ilona Kakai, Mary Kyohere, Andrew Edielu, Fortunate Ambangira, Brenda Kakayi, Valerie Tusubira
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Measles remains a major cause of child morbidity and mortality in low-resource settings. Young infants are particularly at risk of severe disease, with many lacking protective levels of maternal antibodies by six months. Vaccination before nine months in high-burden settings may confer earlier protection; however, concerns exist about its effectiveness at this age. We present early findings on enrolment, safety, and measles infections following the administration of a registered measles-containing vaccine at six months versus nine months (MR1) with a subsequent booster (MR2) at 12 or 18 months of age. Methods We conducted an open-label randomised controlled non-inferiority trial at four health facilities in Kampala, Uganda. Infants aged 24–28 weeks were randomly assigned to receive MR at six and 12 months (group A), nine and 18 months (group B) or six and 18 months (group C). Infants were electronically randomised in a 1:1:1 ratio using block randomisation of varying sizes, stratified by site, maternal HIV status and baseline haemoglobin level. Caretakers recorded solicited reactions on paper diary cards, with safety-related events evaluated. Findings 450 infants received MR1 and were enrolled. No differences in local or systemic post-vaccination events between the six and nine months MR1 groups were observed [Local: 21·2% versus 21·9% ( p = 0·959), systemic: 29·2% vs. 27·7% ( p = 0·845)]. Most local reactions were mild and within the first four days. The majority (5/6) of hospitalisations followed common childhood illnesses, with one non-vaccine-related death six months post-MR1. Sixteen laboratory-confirmed cases of measles (10) and rubella (6), and three clinical measles cases before MR1 were registered. Most measles cases (70·0%) occurred in group C, with no measles cases registered two weeks post-MR2. Conclusion We provide further evidence on the safety of administering a measles-containing vaccine at six months of age. Trial registration : The trial was registered on 31st October 2024 with Clinicaltrials.gov with the identifier NCT06667206.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a major cause of child morbidity and mortality in low-resource settings, and young infants are particularly at risk of severe disease, with many lacking protective levels of maternal antibodies by six months.
Design and population
We conducted an open-label randomised controlled non-inferiority trial at four health facilities in Kampala, Uganda, where infants aged 24–28 weeks were randomly assigned to receive MR at six and 12 months (group A), nine and 18 months (group B) or six and 18 months (group C).
Core methods
Infants were electronically randomised in a 1:1:1 ratio using block randomisation of varying sizes, stratified by site, maternal HIV status and baseline haemoglobin level, and caretakers recorded solicited reactions on paper diary cards, with safety-related events evaluated.
Main results
450 infants received MR1 and were enrolled; no differences in local or systemic post-vaccination events between the six and nine months MR1 groups were observed [Local: 21·2% versus 21·9% (p = 0·959), systemic: 29·2% vs. 27·7% (p = 0·845)], and most local reactions were mild and within the first four days.
Interpretation and novelty
We provide further evidence on the safety of administering a measles-containing vaccine at six months of age, addressing concerns about its effectiveness at this age.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support earlier vaccination in high-burden settings to potentially confer earlier protection, with no increased safety risks observed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the preliminary nature of the findings and the open-label design, which may introduce bias, though the randomisation and stratification enhance reliability.

跨 疫苗 平台对 麻疹病毒 的独特体液反应开发 2251461

Distinct Humoral Response Development to Measles Virus across Vaccine Platforms 2251461
作者： Kwan Pui Chan, Jacqueline Brockhurst, Liting Liu, Jessica Rubens, Shristi Ghimire, Barney Graham, Guillaume Stewart-Jones, Nicole Baumgarth, Diane E Griffin
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 随着 麻疹 发病率 在全球范围内的增加,阐明保护性体液反应的特征势在必行。方法在本研究中,采用噬菌体展示免疫沉淀测序技术 VirScan 来评估用野生型 (WT) 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 感染恒河猴或用减毒活 MeV 疫苗 (LAMV) 麻疹病毒 (LAMV) 感染恒河猴时产生的抗体的病毒表位覆盖率,三重 麻疹 流行性腮腺炎风疹疫苗 (MMR) 或基于新型重组 MeV 血凝素蛋白的 疫苗 (rMeVH)。接种疫苗的动物还接受 WT MeV 攻击,以在召回期间测量抗体表位反应性。结果 结果表明,WT MeV 感染引发的抗体具有比含有 LAMV 的 疫苗 更广泛的表位反应谱。rMeVH 疫苗 不仅产生窄 H 蛋白特异性抗体谱,而且诱导与 WT MeV 或 LAMV 诱导的特异性不同的特异性。所有接种疫苗的动物在 WT MeV 攻击后都扩大了其 MeV 特异性抗体结合库的范围。MeV 特异性 VarScore 是总体抗原结合的量度,与通过 ELISA、亲合力和中和滴度评估的总 IgG 滴度呈正相关。结论这些结果证明麻疹平台对抗麻疹诱导的体液反应的广度和精细特异性具有巨大影响。资金来源 NIH 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着 麻疹 发病率 在全球范围内的增加,阐明保护性体液反应的特征势在必行。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,恒河猴感染了野生型麻疹病毒,或接种了减毒活麻疹 疫苗、三联麻疹腮腺炎风疹疫苗或基于新型重组麻疹血凝素蛋白的疫苗。
核心方法
采用噬菌体展示免疫沉淀测序技术 (VirScan) 来评估抗体对病毒表位的覆盖情况,并用野生型 麻疹病毒 攻击接种疫苗的动物,以在召回过程中测量抗体表位反应性。
主要结果
野生型 麻疹病毒 感染引发的抗体比含有减毒活 疫苗 的 疫苗 具有更广泛的表位反应性,并且基于重组 麻疹 血凝素蛋白的 疫苗 诱导的特异性与野生型 麻疹病毒 或减毒活病毒诱导的特异性不同疫苗。
解释与创新
这些结果证明了 麻疹 平台对抗 麻疹 诱导的体液反应的广度和精细特异性的巨大影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过强调不同平台如何塑造抗体反应来为 疫苗 设计提供信息,这可能会影响对 麻疹 的保护。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在恒河猴中进行的,由于样本量和动物模型有限,证据强度中等。

Distinct Humoral Response Development to Measles Virus across Vaccine Platforms 2251461

Authors: Kwan Pui Chan, Jacqueline Brockhurst, Liting Liu, Jessica Rubens, Shristi Ghimire, Barney Graham, Guillaume Stewart-Jones, Nicole Baumgarth, Diane E Griffin
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction As measles incidences increase globally, it is imperative to elucidate the features of a protective humoral response. Methods In this study, a phage-display immunoprecipitation sequencing technology, VirScan, was employed to evaluate viral epitope coverage by antibodies raised to infection of rhesus macaques with wild-type (WT) measles virus (MeV), or after vaccination with a live-attenuated MeV vaccine (LAMV), the triple measles mumps rubella vaccine (MMR) or a novel recombinant MeV hemagglutinin protein-based vaccine (rMeVH). Vaccinated animals were also challenged with WT MeV to measure antibody epitope reactivity during a recall. Results The results demonstrate that WT MeV infection elicited antibodies with a broader epitope reactivity profile than LAMV-containing vaccines. The rMeVH vaccine not only resulted in a narrow H-protein specific antibody profile, but induced specificities that were distinct from those induced to WT MeV or LAMV. All vaccinated animals expanded the breadth of their MeV-specific antibody binding repertoires following WT MeV challenge. The MeV-specific VarScore, a measure of overall antigen-binding, was positively correlated with total IgG titers assessed by ELISA, avidity, and neutralizing titer. Conclusion These results demonstrate the large effects of vaccination platforms on the breadth and fine-specificity of anti-measles induced humoral responses. Funding Source NIH Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As measles incidences increase globally, it is imperative to elucidate the features of a protective humoral response.
Design and population
In this study, rhesus macaques were infected with wild-type measles virus or vaccinated with a live-attenuated measles vaccine, the triple measles mumps rubella vaccine, or a novel recombinant measles hemagglutinin protein-based vaccine.
Core methods
Phage-display immunoprecipitation sequencing technology (VirScan) was employed to evaluate viral epitope coverage by antibodies, and vaccinated animals were challenged with wild-type measles virus to measure antibody epitope reactivity during a recall.
Main results
Wild-type measles virus infection elicited antibodies with a broader epitope reactivity profile than live-attenuated vaccine-containing vaccines, and the recombinant measles hemagglutinin protein-based vaccine induced specificities distinct from those induced by wild-type measles virus or live-attenuated vaccines.
Interpretation and novelty
These results demonstrate the large effects of vaccination platforms on the breadth and fine-specificity of anti-measles induced humoral responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform vaccine design by highlighting how different platforms shape antibody responses, which may impact protection against measles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in rhesus macaques, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size and animal model.

北加州 7 岁儿童 麻疹 疫苗 覆盖范围的地理差异

Geographic Variation in Measles Vaccine Coverage Among Children Aged 7 Years in Northern California
作者： Karen B. Jacobson, Kristin Goddard, Joan Bartlett, Sally C. Stephens, Helena I. Archer, Lauren D. Liao, Joshua T. B. Williams, Seohyun Kim, Ousseny Zerbo, Sophia R. Newcomer
摘要中文翻译这项 队列研究 研究评估了北加州 7 岁儿童在县人口普查区水平上包含 麻疹 的 疫苗 覆盖率的变化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决北加州 7 岁儿童中 麻疹 疫苗 覆盖率的地理差异,因为 麻疹 由于其高传播性和 暴发 的潜力,仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
一项 队列研究 研究在北加州进行,重点关注各个县人口普查区的 7 岁儿童,以评估当地层面包含 麻疹 的 疫苗 覆盖范围。
核心方法
该研究采用 队列研究 设计,分析北加州 7 岁儿童的数据,以评估县人口普查区层面的 麻疹 疫苗 覆盖率。
主要结果
研究发现,北加州 7 岁儿童的 麻疹 疫苗 覆盖率存在显着的地理差异,一些人口普查区报告的覆盖率低于其他人口普查区。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了 疫苗 覆盖范围的差异,这可能有助于本地化 麻疹 暴发,为地理分析在公共卫生规划中的重要性提供了一个新颖的视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以提高服务不足地区的 麻疹 疫苗 覆盖率,从而有可能降低 麻疹 的风险并增强疾病控制策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对现有数据的依赖以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于其广阔的地理范围和详细的人口普查区域水平分析,证据是强有力的。

Geographic Variation in Measles Vaccine Coverage Among Children Aged 7 Years in Northern California

Authors: Karen B. Jacobson, Kristin Goddard, Joan Bartlett, Sally C. Stephens, Helena I. Archer, Lauren D. Liao, Joshua T. B. Williams, Seohyun Kim, Ousseny Zerbo, Sophia R. Newcomer
Original abstract (English)
This cohort study assesses the variation in measles-containing vaccine coverage at the county census tract level among children aged 7 years in Northern California.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to address geographic variation in measles vaccine coverage among children aged 7 years in Northern California, as measles remains a significant public health concern due to its high transmissibility and potential for outbreaks.
Design and population
A cohort study was conducted in Northern California, focusing on children aged 7 years across various county census tracts to assess measles-containing vaccine coverage at the local level.
Core methods
The study utilized a cohort design, analyzing data from children aged 7 years in Northern California to evaluate measles vaccine coverage at the county census tract level.
Main results
The study found significant geographic variation in measles vaccine coverage among children aged 7 years in Northern California, with some census tracts reporting lower coverage rates than others.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as highlighting disparities in vaccine coverage that may contribute to localized measles outbreaks, offering a novel perspective on the importance of geographic analysis in public health planning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted public health interventions to improve measles vaccine coverage in underserved areas, potentially reducing the risk of outbreaks and enhancing disease control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on existing data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to its large geographic scope and detailed census tract-level analysis.

大流行后 麻疹 印度尼西亚的复苏:2018-2024 年 34 个省份免疫债务的纵向证据

Post-Pandemic Measles Resurgence in Indonesia: Longitudinal Evidence of Immunisation Debt Across 34 Provinces, 2018–2024
作者： Dwiva Wulan Guri, Anggita Rana Sasmita Lestari
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 大流行扰乱了全球常规免疫服务,产生了免疫债务,可能会推动 麻疹 的复苏。这项纵向生态研究考察了 2018 年至 2024 年印度尼西亚 34-38 个省份的 麻疹 暴发 (KLB) 频率和 MR1 疫苗接种 覆盖范围,分为大流行前(2018-2019)、大流行(2020-2021)和大流行后(2022-2024)阶段。数据取自 2018-2024 年印度尼西亚 Kesehatan 概况。KLB 频率在不同阶段存在显着差异 (Kruskal-Wallis H = 32.530,p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 COVID-19 大流行对 麻疹 免疫服务的影响,并评估 麻疹 由于免疫债务而卷土重来的可能性。这场大流行扰乱了全球常规免疫服务,产生了免疫债务,可能会推动 麻疹 的复苏。
设计与对象
这项纵向生态研究考察了 2018 年至 2024 年印度尼西亚 34-38 个省份的 麻疹 暴发 (KLB) 频率和 MR1 疫苗接种 覆盖范围,分为大流行前(2018-2019)、大流行(2020-2021)和大流行后(2022-2024)阶段。
核心方法
数据提取自 Profil Kesehatan Indonesia 2018-2024,其中提供了指定时间段内各省 麻疹 暴发 频率和 MR1 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的信息。
主要结果
KLB 频率在不同阶段存在显着差异 (Kruskal-Wallis H = 32.530,p. 1)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为大流行对 麻疹 免疫服务产生可衡量影响的证据,强调了免疫债务导致的复苏的可能性。这项研究为大流行相关中断对 麻疹 控制的长期影响提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为 麻疹 麻疹 监测 和免疫补足计划的公共卫生策略提供信息,特别是在受大流行相关服务中断影响的地区。他们强调维持免疫覆盖率以预防 暴发 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其生态设计的限制,可能会掩盖影响麻疹 暴发的个体层面因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于省级汇总数据,而没有详细的个人层面分析。

Post-Pandemic Measles Resurgence in Indonesia: Longitudinal Evidence of Immunisation Debt Across 34 Provinces, 2018–2024

Authors: Dwiva Wulan Guri, Anggita Rana Sasmita Lestari
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic disrupted routine immunisation services globally, creating immunisation debt that may drive measles resurgence. This longitudinal ecological study examined measles outbreak (KLB) frequency and MR1 vaccination coverage across 34–38 Indonesian provinces from 2018 to 2024, divided into pre-pandemic (2018–2019), pandemic (2020–2021), and post-pandemic (2022–2024) phases. Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on measles immunisation services and assess the potential for measles resurgence due to immunisation debt. The pandemic disrupted routine immunisation services globally, creating immunisation debt that may drive measles resurgence.

Scientific and public-health significance

2026 年孟加拉国 麻疹 暴发:对免疫差距和护理缺陷的批评

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies
作者： Syed Abu Hasanth
摘要中文翻译孟加拉国在 5 月中旬达到顶峰的严重 麻疹 麻疹 中进入 2026 年。本文分析了公共卫生部门对 暴发 的反应,并评估了政府对危机的管理。卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies

Authors: Syed Abu Hasanth
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May. This article analyses the public health response to the outbreak and evaluates the government's management of the crisis. Health professionals and measles experts have attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.

Question and background
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May, prompting an analysis of the public health response and government management of the crisis.
Design and population
The study evaluates the public health response to the 2026 measles outbreak in Bangladesh, focusing on the government's management of the crisis.
Core methods
Health professionals and measles experts attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
Main results
The study identifies accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding as contributing factors to the outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a result of systemic failures in immunisation and public health infrastructure, highlighting the novel intersection of nutritional deficiencies and measles vulnerability in the Bangladeshi context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for strengthened routine immunisation programmes, improved public health infrastructure, and targeted nutritional interventions to prevent future measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on expert attributions without primary data collection or statistical analysis, limiting causal inference.

使用自适应神经模糊推理系统 (ANFIS) 和人工神经网络 (ANN) 的 麻疹 预测建模

Measles Prediction Modeling using Adaptive Neuro-Fuzzy Inference System (ANFIS) and Artificial Neural Network (ANN)
作者： Declan Ikechukwu Emegano, Ilker Ozsahin, Prof. Dilber Uzun Ozsahin, Paul Isaac Emeje, İlker Özşahin, Dilber Uzun Ozsahin
摘要中文翻译麻疹 是一种病毒性传染性疾病,具有危及生命的并发症,开始时有 8-12 天的潜伏期,伴有发烧、咳嗽、鼻炎和结膜炎。麻疹病毒 (MeV) 进入 宿主 的肺泡巨噬细胞或其树突状细胞,进行复制,然后扩散到周围表面。因此,我们重点关注使用自适应神经模糊推理系统 (ANFIS) 和人工神经网络 (ANN) 来预测 麻疹。使用决定系数 (R2)、均方根误差 (RMSE)、皮尔逊相关系数 (R) 和百分比偏差 (PBIAS) 作为评估指标来采用和评估模型(ANFIS 和 ANN)。结果表明,ANFIS、ANN 和 MLR 在训练和测试中的 R 值为 0.9810、0.9960 和 0.9750。值得注意的是,ANN 在 RMSE 指标方面的得分较低,训练和测试的值为 0.6790 和 0.6809。此外,训练和测试的 ANN PBIAS 值分别为 0.1080 和 0.1142。总之,ANFIS 和 ANN 在建模 麻疹 事件方面证明了有效性,因此,作为公共卫生和决策者的宝贵工具,特别是在确定增强公共安全的策略方面。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用 ANFIS 和 ANN 开发 麻疹 的预测模型,满足公共卫生决策中对预测 麻疹 事件的有效工具的需求。
设计与对象
该研究采用 AFIS 和 ANN 模型来预测 麻疹 的发生,并使用 R2、RMSE、Pearson 相关系数 (R) 和 PBIAS 等评估指标来评估模型性能。
核心方法
使用决定系数 (R2)、均方根误差 (RMSE)、皮尔逊相关系数 (R) 和百分比偏差 (PBIAS) 来评估模型,以比较它们在预测 麻疹 发生方面的有效性。
主要结果
ANFIS、ANN 和 MLR 在训练和测试中的 R 值分别为 0.9810、0.9960 和 0.9750,其中 ANN 在训练和测试中分别实现了较低的 RMSE 值(0.6790 和 0.6809)和 PBIAS 值(0.1080 和 0.1142)。
解释与创新
作者将 ANFIS 和 ANN 解释为对 麻疹 事件进行建模的有效工具,强调它们的新颖性,是公共卫生和决策者增强公共安全策略的宝贵资源。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 麻疹 事件提供了一个预测框架,可以为公共卫生 麻疹、早期干预策略和资源分配提供信息,以减轻 麻疹 麻疹 的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它依赖于仅抽象的数据,没有详细的方法或在现实环境中进行验证,限制了普遍性。

Measles Prediction Modeling using Adaptive Neuro-Fuzzy Inference System (ANFIS) and Artificial Neural Network (ANN)

Authors: Declan Ikechukwu Emegano, Ilker Ozsahin, Prof. Dilber Uzun Ozsahin, Paul Isaac Emeje, İlker Özşahin, Dilber Uzun Ozsahin
Original abstract (English)
Measles, a viral and communicable illness with life-threatening complications, begins with an 8-12-day incubation period accompanied by fever, cough, coryza, and conjunctivitis. The Measles Virus (MeV) enters the alveolar macrophages of the host or its dendritic cells, replicates, and then spreads to the surrounding surfaces. As a result, we focus on the use of Adaptive Neuro-Fuzzy Inference Systems (ANFIS) and Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) for the prediction of measles. The models (ANFIS and ANN) were employed and evaluated using the coefficient of determination (R2), Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), Pearson correlation coefficient (R), and Percentage Bias (PBIAS) as evaluation metrics. The results showed that ANFIS, ANN, and MLR showed R values of 0.9810, 0.9960, and 0.9750 for both training and test. Remarkably, ANN scored lower in terms of the RMSE metric with values of 0.6790 and 0.6809 for both training and testing. In addition, the ANN PBIAS values were 0.1080 and 0.1142 for both training and testing, respectively. In conclusion, ANFIS and ANN have demonstrated effectiveness in modeling measles occurrences, thus, as a valuable tool for public health and decision makers, especially in determining strategies that will enhance public safety.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop predictive models for measles using ANFIS and ANN, addressing the need for effective tools to forecast measles occurrences for public health decision-making.
Design and population
The study employed ANFIS and ANN models to predict measles occurrences, using evaluation metrics such as R2, RMSE, Pearson correlation coefficient (R), and PBIAS to assess model performance.
Core methods
The models were evaluated using the coefficient of determination (R2), Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), Pearson correlation coefficient (R), and Percentage Bias (PBIAS) to compare their effectiveness in predicting measles occurrences.
Main results
ANFIS, ANN, and MLR showed R values of 0.9810, 0.9960, and 0.9750 for both training and test, with ANN achieving lower RMSE values (0.6790 and 0.6809) and PBIAS values (0.1080 and 0.1142) for training and testing, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted ANFIS and ANN as effective tools for modeling measles occurrences, highlighting their novelty as valuable resources for public health and decision-makers in enhancing public safety strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a predictive framework for measles occurrences, which can inform public health surveillance, early intervention strategies, and resource allocation to mitigate measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate, as it relies on abstract-only data without detailed methodology or validation in real-world settings, limiting generalizability.
