C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

与四个区域病毒学研究所加强印度的大流行防范:就 暴发 应对措施和合作研究优先事项进行利益相关者磋商。

Strengthening India's pandemic preparedness with the four zonal institutes of virology: stakeholder consultations for outbreak response and collaborative research priorities.
作者： Kunal Pise, Babasaheb Tandale, Naveen Kumar, Pradip Barde, Ashok Munivenkatappa, Basavaraj Mathapati, Abhijeet V. Jadhav, Pankaj Rawat, Shrinivasa Basavaraj, Satish Gaikwad
摘要中文翻译背景:由世界银行支持的 Pradhan Mantri Ayushman Bharat 卫生基础设施任务 (PM-ABHIM) 计划正在印度建立四个区域性国家病毒学研究所 (NIV)。确定区域疾病和研究重点至关重要,以便将研究重点放在区域层面。本研究报告了一项区域性病毒性疾病优先排序活动,以支持 暴发 响应和协作研究。它描述了区域优先的病毒性疾病,评估区域优先列表的一致性和差异,并使用基于多标准决策分析(MCDA)的共识过程确定跨领域的研究主题和准备差距。方法:我们于 2024 年 3 月在印度的四个地理区域开展了一项横断面、多利益相关者协商研究。区域级协商研讨会采用了系统性的共识建立方法,使用了修改后的 One Health 人畜共患疾病优先排序 (OHZDP) 工具,该工具适用于新出现的病毒性疾病,并通过 MCDA 应用。我们确定了区域级利益相关者,列出了 40 种病毒性疾病,并将其分为三组:常见发生(A 组)、有限发生(B 组)和有机会出现、进化 或输入的罕见发生(C 组)。来自州卫生部门、医学院、研究机构和病毒研究与诊断实验室(VRDL)网络的利益相关者对每个区域的病毒性疾病和研究主题进行了排名。斯皮尔曼排名相关性用于评估区域之间优先级排名的一致性。结果:共有 186 名参与者(每个区域 42-48 人)参与了四个区域研讨会,其中包括 VRDL 实验室的合作者 (26%)、综合疾病 监测 计划 (IDSP) 的公共卫生利益相关者 (28%)、特邀专家 (21%)、组织者 (19%) 和印度医学研究委员会 (ICMR) 代表 (6%)。主要成果包括 A、B 和 C 组中特定区域的优先病毒性疾病清单。病毒性疾病优先顺序存在区域差异,反映了当地的流行病学模式。Spearman 等级相关分析显示中部和东部区域之间呈中度正相关(rho = 0.697,P = 0.031),而其他成对比较没有统计显着性,这表明区域内既有共同的又有不同的优先模式。各地区排名前五位的重点病毒性疾病包括登革热、流感、麻疹、日本脑炎和甲型肝炎。然后确定合作研究的研究主题和副主题。结论:这些多利益相关方磋商提供了一个新颖的、可复制的模板,用于优先考虑病毒性疾病,以制定通过合作研究减轻未来 暴发 影响的策略。这些区域优先事项可能会指导未来在国家、区域和全球层面的类似活动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定区域性病毒性疾病优先事项,以支持印度的 暴发 应对和合作研究,解决将研究工作集中在区域一级的需要。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月,在印度四个地理区域开展了一项跨部门、多利益相关者协商研究,涉及来自邦卫生部门、医学院、研究机构和 VRDL 网络的 186 名参与者。
核心方法
该研究使用了基于多标准决策分析 (MCDA) 的共识流程,以及适用于新出现的病毒性疾病的经过修改的 One Health 人畜共患疾病优先排序工具,以及 Spearman 等级相关性来评估区域之间的一致性。
主要结果
四十种病毒性疾病被分为三组(常见、有限、罕见/新兴),优先顺序的区域差异反映了当地的流行病学模式;各区的前五个优先重点是登革热、流感、麻疹、日本脑炎和甲型肝炎。
解释与创新
作者将多利益相关方磋商解释为一种新颖的、可复制的模板,用于优先考虑病毒性疾病,以通过合作研究缓解未来的 暴发。
科研与公卫意义
区域优先事项可以指导国家、区域和全球层面的类似活动,帮助制定 暴发 响应和合作研究战略。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于基于共识的方法,优先级存在区域差异,尽管样本量和方法对于优先级划分来说是稳健的。

Strengthening India's pandemic preparedness with the four zonal institutes of virology: stakeholder consultations for outbreak response and collaborative research priorities.

Authors: Kunal Pise, Babasaheb Tandale, Naveen Kumar, Pradip Barde, Ashok Munivenkatappa, Basavaraj Mathapati, Abhijeet V. Jadhav, Pankaj Rawat, Shrinivasa Basavaraj, Satish Gaikwad
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The Pradhan Mantri Ayushman Bharat Health Infrastructure Mission (PM-ABHIM) scheme, supported by the World Bank, is establishing four zonal National Institutes of Virology (NIVs) in India. It is essential to identify the zonal disease and research priorities so as to focus research at zonal levels. This study reports a zonal viral disease prioritisation exercise to support outbreak response and collaborative research. It describes zonal-prioritised viral diseases, assesses concordance and differences in zonal priority lists and, and identifies cross‐cutting research themes and preparedness gaps using an multicriteria decision analysis (MCDA)‐based consensus process. METHODS: We conducted a cross-sectional, multi-stakeholder consultative study across four geographical zones of India in March 2024. The zonal-level consultative workshops employed a systematic consensus-building approach using the modified One Health Zoonotic Disease Prioritisation (OHZDP) tool, adapted for emerging viral diseases and applied via MCDA. We identified zonal-level stakeholders, listed 40 viral diseases and categorised them into three groups: common occurrence (Group A), limited occurrence (Group B), and rare occurrences with a chance of emergence, evolution or importation (Group C). The stakeholders from state health departments, medical colleges, research institutes and the Virus Research and Diagnostic Laboratory (VRDL) network ranked the viral diseases and research themes in each zone. Spearman rank correlation was used to assess concordance of priority rankings between zones. RESULTS: A total of 186 participants (42-48 per zone) contributed to the four zonal workshops including collaborators from VRDL laboratories (26%), public health stakeholders from Integrated Disease Surveillance programme(IDSP) (28%), invited experts (21%), organisers (19%) and Indian Council of Medical Research (ICMR) representatives (6%). The key outcomes included the zone-specific lists of priority viral diseases in Groups A, B and C. There were zonal variations in viral disease prioritisation, reflecting local epidemiological patterns. Spearman rank correlation analysis showed moderate positive correlation between the Central and East zones (rho = 0.697, P = 0.031), whereas other pairwise comparisons were not statistically significant, which indicated both shared and distinct priority patterns within the zones. The top five priority viral diseases across zones included dengue, influenza, measles, Japanese encephalitis and hepatitis A. The research themes and subthemes were then decided for collaborative research. CONCLUSIONS: These multistakeholder consultations provided a novel, replicable template for prioritising viral diseases to develop strategies for mitigating the impact of future outbreaks through collaborative research. These zonal priorities may guide similar exercises at national, regional and global levels in future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify zonal viral disease priorities to support outbreak response and collaborative research in India, addressing the need to focus research efforts at zonal levels.
Design and population
A cross-sectional, multi-stakeholder consultative study was conducted across four geographical zones of India in March 2024, involving 186 participants from state health departments, medical colleges, research institutes, and the VRDL network.
Core methods
The study used a multicriteria decision analysis (MCDA)-based consensus process with a modified One Health Zoonotic Disease Prioritisation tool adapted for emerging viral diseases, and Spearman rank correlation to assess concordance between zones.
Main results
Forty viral diseases were categorized into three groups (common, limited, rare/emerging), with zonal variations in prioritization reflecting local epidemiological patterns; the top five priorities across zones were dengue, influenza, measles, Japanese encephalitis, and hepatitis A.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the multistakeholder consultations as a novel, replicable template for prioritizing viral diseases to mitigate future outbreaks through collaborative research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The zonal priorities may guide similar exercises at national, regional, and global levels, helping to develop strategies for outbreak response and collaborative research.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relied on a consensus-based approach with zonal variations in prioritization, though the sample size and methodology were robust for a prioritization exercise.

9-12 岁接种 MMR 疫苗的儿童对流行性腮腺炎的血清保护作用:一项横断面研究。

Seroprotection Against Mumps in MMR Vaccinated Children Aged 9-12 Years: A Cross-Sectional Study.
作者： Dhairya Bansal, Aaradhana, Bineeta Kashyap, Anju Aggarwal, Debamita Banik
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在估计在儿童早期施用单剂 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗 后具有针对腮腺炎血清保护性抗体滴度的 9-12 岁儿童的比例,并探讨血清保护与临床人口统计学特征之间的关联。
设计与对象
2024 年 4 月至 2025 年 8 月期间,在德里的一家三级医院进行了一项横断面研究。招募了 9-12 岁、有记录在 16-24 个月大时接受过单剂 MMR 疫苗 的儿童。
核心方法
收集 2 mL 静脉血,并使用酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 分析血清腮腺炎特异性 IgG 抗体,血清保护定义为腮腺炎抗体滴度≥ 22 RU/mL。使用卡方检验或 Fisher 精确检验评估血清保护与人口统计和临床变量(包括年龄、性别、疫苗接种 年龄和营养状况)之间的关联。
主要结果
纳入了 86 名 9-12 岁儿童(48% 男性)[平均 (SD) 10.35 (1.04) 岁]。总体而言,84.8% 的人表现出针对腮腺炎的血清保护性抗体滴度。中位(q1,q3)腮腺炎特异性 IgG 滴度为 97.6(46.1,144.9)RU/mL。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需要对流行性腮腺炎免疫力的持久性进行更大规模的社区研究,并为印度未来的 MMR 疫苗接种 战略提供越来越多的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了有关接种 MMR 疫苗的儿童对流行性腮腺炎的长期血清保护作用的见解,这可以为印度未来的 疫苗接种 战略和 监测 工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一家三级医院进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于横截面设计和有限的样本量。

Seroprotection Against Mumps in MMR Vaccinated Children Aged 9-12 Years: A Cross-Sectional Study.

Authors: Dhairya Bansal, Aaradhana, Bineeta Kashyap, Anju Aggarwal, Debamita Banik
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: To estimate the proportion of children aged 9-12 years with seroprotective antibody titers against mumps following a single dose of measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccine administered in early childhood, and to explore the association between seroprotection and clinicodemographic profile. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted at a tertiary hospital in Delhi between April 2024 and August 2025. Children aged 9-12 years with a documented receipt of a single dose of MMR vaccine at 16-24 months of age were recruited. Two mL of venous blood was collected and analyzed for serum mumps-specific IgG antibodies using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), with seroprotection defined as mumps antibody titers ≥ 22 RU/mL. The association between seroprotection and demographic and clinical variables, including age, sex, age at vaccination, and nutritional status, was assessed using the chi-square test or Fisher's exact test. RESULTS: Eighty-six children (48% males) aged 9-12 years [mean (SD) 10.35 (1.04) years] were enrolled. Overall, 84.8% demonstrated seroprotective antibody titers against mumps. The median (q1, q3) mumps-specific IgG titer was 97.6 (46.1, 144.9) RU/mL. Children aged > 10 years were significantly more likely to be seronegative compared to younger children (OR 3.99, 95% CI 1.07, 14.92, P = 0.039). CONCLUSION: Nearly 85% of children who had received a single MMR dose at 16-24 months of age had seroprotective mumps antibody titers at 9-12 years. The findings highlight the need for larger community-based studies on the durability of mumps immunity and add to the accumulating evidence informing future MMR vaccination strategies in India.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the proportion of children aged 9-12 years with seroprotective antibody titers against mumps following a single dose of measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccine administered in early childhood, and to explore the association between seroprotection and clinicodemographic profile.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted at a tertiary hospital in Delhi between April 2024 and August 2025. Children aged 9-12 years with a documented receipt of a single dose of MMR vaccine at 16-24 months of age were recruited.
Core methods
Two mL of venous blood was collected and analyzed for serum mumps-specific IgG antibodies using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), with seroprotection defined as mumps antibody titers ≥ 22 RU/mL. The association between seroprotection and demographic and clinical variables, including age, sex, age at vaccination, and nutritional status, was assessed using the chi-square test or Fisher's exact test.
Main results
Eighty-six children (48% males) aged 9-12 years [mean (SD) 10.35 (1.04) years] were enrolled. Overall, 84.8% demonstrated seroprotective antibody titers against mumps. The median (q1, q3) mumps-specific IgG titer was 97.6 (46.1, 144.9) RU/mL.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for larger community-based studies on the durability of mumps immunity and add to the accumulating evidence informing future MMR vaccination strategies in India.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the long-term seroprotection against mumps in children vaccinated with MMR, which can inform future vaccination strategies and surveillance efforts in India.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted at a single tertiary hospital, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a cross-sectional design and limited sample size.

Association Between First MMR Vaccination Before Age 2 Years and Childhood Autism in a U.S. EHR Cohort of 2.5 Million Children.

Association Between First MMR Vaccination Before Age 2 Years and Childhood Autism in a U.S. EHR Cohort of 2.5 Million Children.
作者： Todd L Burstain, Dale Jacques, Jennifer M. Burstain
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管先前进行了大量研究,但对 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 与自闭症之间潜在关联的担忧仍然存在。评估 疫苗 时机的观察性研究很容易受到医疗保健利用模式、神经发育问题和时间依赖性暴露分类的偏差的影响。方法:我们使用 Cosmos 电子健康记录数据库进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,包括 2,560,035 名美国儿童,这些儿童具有相关的出生父母数据并随访至 8 岁。为了最大限度地减少与暴露时间和医疗保健利用相关的偏差,我们进行了预先指定的基于年龄的标志性分析,评估了 11.5 至 24 个月之间施用的第一次 MMR 疫苗接种。使用 Cox 比例风险模型和通过有向无环图选择的协变量来估计自闭症的调整风险比 (aHR)。分析阴性对照暴露(在 12-15 个月施用肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 加强剂)以评估残留混杂因素。结果:在主要的 11.5-24 个月里程碑 队列研究 中,第一个 MMR 疫苗接种 与自闭症风险增加无关(aHR 0.97;99% 置信区间 0.91-1.03)。在所有年龄特定标志 队列研究 中,aHR 仍接近零。阴性对照分析得出了类似的接近零的估计值(aHR 1.01;99% 置信区间 0.99-1.04),支持残余偏差不太可能解释研究结果。结论:在这项大型美国 队列研究 中,年龄锚定的里程碑分析显示 24 个月前的 MMR 队列研究 与儿童自闭症之间没有关联。MMR 和阴性对照暴露的一致近乎零结果支持观察到的结果不太可能用残留混杂因素来解释。这些发现强调了年龄特异性分析方法在使用观察数据评估 疫苗 安全性方面的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管先前进行了大量研究,但对 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 与自闭症之间潜在关联的担忧仍然存在。
设计与对象
我们使用 Cosmos 电子健康记录数据库进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,其中包括 2,560,035 名美国儿童,这些儿童具有关联的出生父母数据并随访至 8 岁。
核心方法
为了最大限度地减少与暴露时间和医疗保健利用相关的偏差,我们进行了预先指定的基于年龄的标志性分析,评估了 11.5 至 24 个月之间施用的第一次 MMR 疫苗接种,并使用 Cox 比例风险模型和通过有向无环图选择的协变量来估计自闭症的调整后风险比 (aHR)。
主要结果
在主要的 11.5-24 个月标志点 队列研究 中,第一个 MMR 队列研究 与自闭症风险增加无关(aHR 0.97;99% 置信区间 0.91-1.03),并且在所有年龄特定标志点 队列研究 中,aHR 仍接近零。
解释与创新
在这项大型美国 队列研究 中,年龄锚定的里程碑分析显示 24 个月前的 MMR 疫苗 与儿童自闭症之间没有关联,这些发现强调了年龄特异性分析方法在使用观察数据评估 疫苗 安全性方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了强有力的证据,证明早期 MMR 疫苗接种 与自闭症之间存在关联,支持当前的 疫苗接种 指南并为公共卫生沟通策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
MMR 和阴性对照暴露的一致近乎零结果支持观察到的结果不太可能用残留混杂因素来解释,由于样本量大和分析方法严格,证据强度高。

Association Between First MMR Vaccination Before Age 2 Years and Childhood Autism in a U.S. EHR Cohort of 2.5 Million Children.

Authors: Todd L Burstain, Dale Jacques, Jennifer M. Burstain
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Concerns about potential associations between measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccination and autism persist despite extensive prior research. Observational studies evaluating vaccine timing are susceptible to bias from healthcare utilization patterns, neurodevelopmental concerns and time-dependent exposure classification. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study using the Cosmos electronic health record database, including 2,560,035 US children with linked birth-parent data and follow-up through age 8 years. To minimize bias related to exposure timing and healthcare utilization, we performed prespecified age-based landmark analyses evaluating the first MMR vaccination administered between 11.5 and 24 months. Adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for autism were estimated using Cox proportional hazards models with covariates selected via directed acyclic graphs. A negative control exposure (pneumococcal conjugate vaccine booster administered at 12-15 months) was analyzed to assess residual confounding. RESULTS: In the primary 11.5-24 month landmark cohort, the first MMR vaccination was not associated with increased autism risk (aHR 0.97; 99% confidence interval 0.91-1.03). Across all age-specific landmark cohorts, aHRs remained near the null. The negative control analysis yielded a similarly near-null estimate (aHR 1.01; 99% confidence interval 0.99-1.04), supporting that residual bias is unlikely to explain the findings. CONCLUSIONS: In this large US cohort, age-anchored landmark analyses show no association between MMR vaccination before 24 months and childhood autism. The concordant near-null findings for both MMR and a negative control exposure support that the observed results are unlikely to be explained by residual confounding. These findings highlight the importance of age-specific analytic approaches in evaluating vaccine safety using observational data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Concerns about potential associations between measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccination and autism persist despite extensive prior research.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective cohort study using the Cosmos electronic health record database, including 2,560,035 US children with linked birth-parent data and follow-up through age 8 years.
Core methods
To minimize bias related to exposure timing and healthcare utilization, we performed prespecified age-based landmark analyses evaluating the first MMR vaccination administered between 11.5 and 24 months, and adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for autism were estimated using Cox proportional hazards models with covariates selected via directed acyclic graphs.
Main results
In the primary 11.5-24 month landmark cohort, the first MMR vaccination was not associated with increased autism risk (aHR 0.97; 99% confidence interval 0.91-1.03), and across all age-specific landmark cohorts, aHRs remained near the null.
Interpretation and novelty
In this large US cohort, age-anchored landmark analyses show no association between MMR vaccination before 24 months and childhood autism, and these findings highlight the importance of age-specific analytic approaches in evaluating vaccine safety using observational data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide robust evidence against an association between early MMR vaccination and autism, supporting current vaccination guidelines and informing public health communication strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The concordant near-null findings for both MMR and a negative control exposure support that the observed results are unlikely to be explained by residual confounding, with high evidence strength due to the large sample size and rigorous analytic approach.

溶瘤病毒表达 BiTE 的计算机模拟临床试验揭示了患者异质性对剂量方案的影响。

In silico clinical trials of BiTE expression by oncolytic viruses reveal the impact of patient heterogeneity on dosage protocol.
作者： Adrianne L. Jenner, Robyn P. Araujo, Noa L. Levi, Guy Ungerechts, Christine E. Engeland, Johannes P.W. Heidbuechel
摘要中文翻译近年来,免疫疗法已成为许多癌症类型的变革性治疗策略。双特异性 T 细胞接合剂 (BiTE) 是一种有前景的免疫疗法,可通过将 T 细胞重新导向癌细胞来增强细胞抗肿瘤免疫力。最近的证据表明,通过在溶瘤 麻疹病毒 媒介 (MV-BiTE) 中编码 BiTE 可以增强 BiTE 功效。用 MV-BiTE 感染癌细胞会导致 BiTE 的局部产生,并在小鼠肿瘤模型中显示出安全性和有效性。然而,观察到的 治疗 功效是否会转化为异质人群尚不清楚。在这项工作中,我们使用常微分方程组进行了 MV-BiTE 疗法的计算机临床试验。我们利用 II 期临床试验中体内肿瘤体积的变化和基线 (%) 测量值的变化来捕获个体患者的潜在异质性。我们使用溶瘤病毒 talimogene laherparepvec (T-VEC) 作为携带免疫刺激有效负载的替代溶瘤病毒来代替人类 MV-BiTE 数据。我们的预测表明异质性的主要驱动因素是潜在的效应 T 细胞杀伤率和 BiTE 药代动力学。此外,我们发现,如果个体被归类为对 II 期 T-VEC 临床方案无反应的人,他们可能会对更频繁、更低剂量的给药产生反应。这项工作强调了计算机临床试验如何为新疗法提供预测,以生成假设并指导 治疗 临床转化时间表的设计。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定在小鼠肿瘤模型中观察到的 MV-BiTE 疗法的疗效是否可以转化为异质人群,从而解决临床转化中的关键知识差距。
设计与对象
使用常微分方程系统进行了一项计算机临床试验,根据 II 期临床试验的肿瘤体积和基线测量结果对患者异质性进行建模。
核心方法
研究人员使用常微分方程进行了一项计算机模拟临床试验,并使用 T-VEC 作为替代溶瘤病毒,通过改变肿瘤体积和基线测量来纳入患者异质性。
主要结果
这些预测将效应 T 细胞杀伤率和 BiTE 药代动力学确定为异质性的主要驱动因素,并表明对 II 期 T-VEC 方案无反应的人可能会对更频繁地施用较低剂量有反应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,计算机临床试验可以为新疗法提供预测,以生成假设并指导 治疗 临床转化时间表,凸显了这种方法的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 MV-BiTE 治疗的个性化 治疗 方案的设计提供信息,有可能提高异质患者群体的疗效,并推进基于溶瘤病毒的免疫疗法的临床转化。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖替代数据 (T-VEC) 且缺乏直接的人类 MV-BiTE 数据,证据强度中等,尽管计算机方法允许系统探索患者异质性。

In silico clinical trials of BiTE expression by oncolytic viruses reveal the impact of patient heterogeneity on dosage protocol.

Authors: Adrianne L. Jenner, Robyn P. Araujo, Noa L. Levi, Guy Ungerechts, Christine E. Engeland, Johannes P.W. Heidbuechel
Original abstract (English)
Immunotherapies have become a transformative therapeutic strategy for many cancer types in recent years. Bispecific T-cell engagers (BiTEs) are one promising immunotherapy that enhances cellular antitumour immunity by redirecting T cells towards cancer cells. Recent evidence suggests that BiTE efficacy can be augmented by encoding BiTEs in oncolytic measles virus vectors (MV-BiTE). Infection of cancer cells with MV-BiTE causes the local production of BiTEs and has shown safety and efficacy in murine tumour models. However, whether the observed efficacy of this treatment will translate to a heterogeneous human population is unknown. In this work, we generate an in silico clinical trial of MV-BiTE therapy using a system of ordinary differential equations. We capture potential heterogeneity of individual patients using variability in in vivo tumour volume and change in baseline (%) measurements from a Phase II clinical trial. In lieu of human MV-BiTE data, we use the oncolytic virus talimogene laherparepvec (T-VEC) as a surrogate oncolytic virus carrying an immunostimulatory payload. Our predictions imply that the main drivers of heterogeneity are the underlying effector T cell killing rate and BiTE pharmacokinetics. Furthermore, we find that if individuals are classified as non-responders to the Phase II T-VEC clinical protocol, they may respond to more frequent administration of lower dosages. This work highlights how in silico clinical trials can provide predictions for novel therapeutics to generate hypotheses and guide the design of treatment schedules for clinical translation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine whether the efficacy of MV-BiTE therapy observed in murine tumour models would translate to a heterogeneous human population, addressing a critical knowledge gap in clinical translation.
Design and population
An in silico clinical trial was conducted using a system of ordinary differential equations to model patient heterogeneity based on tumour volume and baseline measurements from a Phase II clinical trial.
Core methods
The researchers generated an in silico clinical trial using ordinary differential equations and incorporated patient heterogeneity by varying tumour volume and baseline measurements, using T-VEC as a surrogate oncolytic virus.
Main results
The predictions identified effector T cell killing rate and BiTE pharmacokinetics as main drivers of heterogeneity, and suggested that non-responders to the Phase II T-VEC protocol may respond to more frequent administration of lower dosages.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that in silico clinical trials can provide predictions for novel therapeutics to generate hypotheses and guide treatment schedules for clinical translation, highlighting the novelty of this approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the design of personalized treatment schedules for MV-BiTE therapy, potentially improving efficacy in heterogeneous patient populations and advancing the clinical translation of oncolytic virus-based immunotherapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on surrogate data (T-VEC) and the absence of direct human MV-BiTE data, though the in silico approach allows for systematic exploration of patient heterogeneity.

Journal: Emergency medicine Australasia : EMA  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/1742-6723.70313  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 38, 4, e70313

旧的又是新的:识别和治疗 疫苗-急诊科儿童可预防疾病的提示和技巧。

What Is Old Is New Again: Tips and Tricks for Recognizing and Treating Vaccine-Preventable Diseases of Childhood Presenting to the Emergency Department.
作者： Dinithi Seneviratne, Roland Semakula
摘要中文翻译在全球范围内,疫苗 可预防疾病再次出现,反映出免疫力下降、疫苗 犹豫不决以及 疫苗 覆盖范围的差距。麻疹 被视为 20 世纪最成功的公共卫生干预措施之一——结果,许多 疫苗 可预防的疾病,如 麻疹 和白喉,被降级到教科书。随着 疫苗 可预防疾病 (VPD) 的重新出现,为急诊医生提供快速识别和管理潜在病例的知识并加强公共卫生措施以遏制 传播 已成为当务之急。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决全球范围内 疫苗 可预防疾病的死灰复燃问题,原因是免疫力下降、疫苗 犹豫不决以及 疫苗 覆盖范围的差距。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 麻疹病毒、麻疹 麻疹病毒和人麻疹病毒,重点关注 麻疹 疾病和 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 作为相关实体。
核心方法
该研究涉及考察 麻疹病毒 的历史背景及其复兴,对急诊医学和公共卫生干预措施的影响。
主要结果
该研究强调了 麻疹病毒 及相关疾病的重新出现,强调急诊医生需要有效地识别和管理这些疾病。
解释与创新
作者将 麻疹病毒 的复兴解释为对医疗保健专业人员采取行动的呼吁,以提高他们的诊断和管理技能,这是现代公共卫生挑战背景下的一种新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了维持 疫苗接种 计划和改善急诊科准备工作以应对 麻疹病毒 和相关疾病死灰复燃的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于抽象级别的证据,这可能会限制分析的深度,但其含义是明确的,并且与主题相关,具有中等的置信度。

What Is Old Is New Again: Tips and Tricks for Recognizing and Treating Vaccine-Preventable Diseases of Childhood Presenting to the Emergency Department.

Authors: Dinithi Seneviratne, Roland Semakula
Original abstract (English)
Across the globe, there has been a resurgence in vaccine-preventable diseases reflecting declining immunity, vaccine hesitancy and gaps in vaccine coverage. Vaccination was seen as one of the most successful public health interventions of the 20th century-as a consequence, many vaccine-preventable diseases such as measles and diphtheria were relegated to textbooks. With the re-emergence of vaccine-preventable diseases (VPDs), it is an urgent priority to equip emergency physicians with the knowledge to rapidly identify and manage potential cases and strengthen public health measures to curb transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the resurgence of vaccine-preventable diseases globally, which is attributed to declining immunity, vaccine hesitancy, and gaps in vaccine coverage.
Design and population
The study focused on measles virus, measles morbillivirus, and Morbillivirus hominis, with an emphasis on measles disease and subacute sclerosing panencephalitis as related entities.
Core methods
The study involved examining the historical context of measles virus and its resurgence, with implications for emergency medicine and public health interventions.
Main results
The study highlighted the re-emergence of measles virus and related diseases, emphasizing the need for emergency physicians to recognize and manage these conditions effectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the resurgence of measles virus as a call to action for healthcare professionals to enhance their diagnostic and management skills, which is a novel approach in the context of modern public health challenges.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of maintaining vaccination programs and improving emergency department preparedness to address the resurgence of measles virus and related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-level evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis, but the implications are clear and relevant to the topic with a moderate confidence level.

数字健康传播崩溃:公众信任、疫苗 短缺以及 Surokkha 应用程序的关闭

Digital Health Communication Collapse: Public Trust, Vaccine Shortages, and the Shutdown of the Surokkha App
作者： Mustak Ahmed
摘要中文翻译摘要:在 COVID-19 大流行期间引入数字健康平台是改善公共卫生治理的重要一步,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。Surokkha 应用程序是孟加拉国用于促进 COVID-19 疫苗接种 活动的数字工具之一。它允许注册预约、获取 疫苗接种 证书并提供基本的健康信息。然而,由于有报道称儿童常规 疫苗(包括 麻疹)短缺,该工具被终止,这引发了公众的广泛困惑和不信任。本研究旨在确定数字工具的停用和传统 疫苗 的短缺如何削弱政府有效沟通和恢复信心的能力。该研究从数字治理、风险沟通和政策实施的角度审视了这场危机。它评估有关该问题的政策文件、专家意见和媒体报道,以确定值得关注的关键领域。调查结果显示,这场危机是由于数字健康治理不足、应急计划不力以及风险沟通不力造成的,从而加剧了恐慌和不信任。该研究的结论是,有效的数字健康治理需要机构协调、应急计划和有效的公众沟通,以建立信任并确保可持续的政策成果。汲取的经验教训可以帮助其他发展中国家应对类似的挑战,并有效地从紧急情况过渡到可持续的卫生治理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了 Surokkha 应用程序的停用和传统 疫苗 的短缺如何影响孟加拉国在 COVID-19 疫苗接种 活动期间政府有效沟通和恢复公众信心的能力。
设计与对象
该研究是对孟加拉国有关 Surokkha 应用程序停用和 麻疹 疫苗 短缺的政策文件、专家意见和媒体报道的定性分析。
核心方法
该研究评估政策文件、专家意见和媒体报道,以确定与数字健康治理、风险沟通和政策实施相关的关键关注领域。
主要结果
调查结果显示,这场危机是由于数字健康治理不足、应急计划不力以及风险沟通不力造成的,从而加剧了恐慌和不信任。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为需要机构协调、应急计划和有效的公众沟通来建立信任并确保可持续的政策成果的证据。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了数字健康治理和风险沟通的挑战,可以为发展中国家维护公众信任和有效政策实施的战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,依赖于政策文件、专家意见和媒体报道的定性分析,这可能会引入偏见并限制研究结果的强度。

Digital Health Communication Collapse: Public Trust, Vaccine Shortages, and the Shutdown of the Surokkha App

Authors: Mustak Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The introduction of digital health platforms during the COVID-19 pandemic was a significant step toward improving public health governance, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. The Surokkha App was one of the digital tools used in Bangladesh to facilitate the COVID-19 vaccination campaign. It allowed registering appointments, obtaining vaccination certificates, and providing essential health information. However, the tool was discontinued amid reports of a shortage of routine childhood vaccines, including measles, which triggered widespread confusion and mistrust among the public. This study aims to determine how the discontinuation of the digital tool and the shortage of conventional vaccines undermined the government’s ability to communicate effectively and restore confidence. The study examines the crisis through the lens of digital governance, risk communication, and policy implementation. It evaluates policy documents, experts’ opinions, and media reports on the issue to identify the critical areas of concern. The findings reveal that the crisis was caused by inadequate digital health governance, poor contingency planning, and ineffective risk communication, which fueled panic and mistrust. The study concludes that effective digital health governance requires institutional coordination, contingency planning, and effective public communication to build trust and ensure sustainable policy outcomes. The lessons learned can help other developing countries navigate similar challenges and transition from emergency to sustainable health governance effectively.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates how the discontinuation of the Surokkha App and the shortage of conventional vaccines impacted the government’s ability to communicate effectively and restore public confidence during the COVID-19 vaccination campaign in Bangladesh.
Design and population
The study is a qualitative analysis of policy documents, experts’ opinions, and media reports on the discontinuation of the Surokkha App and the measles vaccine shortage in Bangladesh.
Core methods
The study evaluates policy documents, experts’ opinions, and media reports to identify critical areas of concern related to digital health governance, risk communication, and policy implementation.
Main results
The findings reveal that the crisis was caused by inadequate digital health governance, poor contingency planning, and ineffective risk communication, which fueled panic and mistrust.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence of the need for institutional coordination, contingency planning, and effective public communication to build trust and ensure sustainable policy outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the challenges of digital health governance and risk communication, which can inform strategies for maintaining public trust and effective policy implementation in developing countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence and relies on qualitative analysis of policy documents, experts’ opinions, and media reports, which may introduce bias and limit the strength of the findings.

父母作为儿童支持者和反对者的角色 疫苗接种:全国代表性调查的结果

Parents’ role as advocates for and against childhood vaccination: Findings from a nationally representative survey
作者： Mallory K. Ellingson, Tara L. Queen, Melissa B. Gilkey, Noel T. Brewer, Emma H. Elias, Kathryn L. Kennedy, William A. Calo, Nora E. Rosenberg
摘要中文翻译简介:2025 年,美国记录了 25 年来最高的 麻疹 病例数,表明迫切需要重新关注增加儿童 麻疹 覆盖率。为了确定未来的干预合作伙伴,我们试图了解父母在支持和反对儿童 疫苗 方面的角色。方法:全国范围内具有代表性的 1,315 名 9-12 岁儿童家长样本参与了我们关于 2025 年 疫苗 倡导行为的在线调查。单独的加权逻辑回归模型确定了鼓励或阻止其他家长为孩子常规使用 疫苗 的相关性。结果:超过三分之一的家长表示有时或更频繁地鼓励常规 疫苗 (36%),而约十分之一的家长表示不鼓励 疫苗 (12%)。这些估计包括一小部分报告这两种行为的父母 (6%)。对 疫苗 对孩子健康的重要性有较高信心的父母更有可能成为鼓励者,而不太可能成为劝阻者(均 p < .05)。鼓励者 (n = 477) 最常提倡 麻疹 (50%) 或季节性流感 疫苗 (44%)。这些父母最常与家人和亲密朋友接触,使用的策略包括分享 疫苗 经验 (47%)、有关 疫苗 有效性的信息 (38%) 以及有关 疫苗 的建议或意见 (35%)。劝阻者 (n = 151) 最常主张反对 Covid-19 (53%) 或季节性流感 疫苗 (41%),相对较少的人关注 麻疹 疫苗 (10%)。劝阻者的受众和策略与鼓励者相似。结论:我们的全国代表性研究结果表明,鼓励常规 疫苗接种 的父母远远多于不鼓励这样做的父母。鉴于鼓励者经常提倡 麻疹 疫苗,未来增加 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的努力应考虑如何与家长倡导者合作,以阻止这种疾病令人震惊的卷土重来。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025 年,美国记录了 25 年来最高的 麻疹 病例数,这表明迫切需要重新关注增加儿童 麻疹 覆盖率。本研究旨在了解父母在支持和反对儿童 疫苗 方面的角色,以确定未来的干预合作伙伴。
设计与对象
2025 年,全国有代表性的 1,315 名 9-12 岁儿童家长参与了一项关于 疫苗 倡导行为的在线调查。该研究重点关注家长对 麻疹 疫苗 的行为,这是鼓励者最常倡导的。
核心方法
单独的加权逻辑回归模型确定了鼓励或阻止其他父母为孩子进行常规 疫苗 的相关性。分析包括检查家长在宣传工作中使用的策略和受众。
主要结果
超过三分之一的家长表示有时或更频繁地鼓励常规 疫苗 (36%),而约十分之一的家长则表示不鼓励 疫苗 (12%)。这些估计包括一小部分报告这两种行为的父母 (6%)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,鼓励常规 疫苗接种 的父母远远多于不鼓励的父母。该研究强调了了解影响父母 疫苗 倡导行为的因素的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于鼓励者经常提倡 麻疹 疫苗,未来增加 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的努力应考虑如何与家长倡导者合作,以阻止这种疾病令人震惊的卷土重来。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于家长自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏见。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于调查反应而不是直接的生物学或临床测量。

Parents’ role as advocates for and against childhood vaccination: Findings from a nationally representative survey

Authors: Mallory K. Ellingson, Tara L. Queen, Melissa B. Gilkey, Noel T. Brewer, Emma H. Elias, Kathryn L. Kennedy, William A. Calo, Nora E. Rosenberg
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: In 2025, the US recorded the highest number of measles cases in 25 years, signaling an urgent need for renewed attention to increasing childhood vaccination coverage. To identify future intervention partners, we sought to understand parents' role in advocating for and against childhood vaccines. METHODS: A nationally representative sample of 1,315 parents of children ages 9-12 years participated in our online survey about vaccine advocacy behaviors in 2025. Separate weighted logistic regression models identified correlates of encouraging or discouraging other parents from getting routine vaccines for their children. RESULTS: Over one-third of parents reported encouraging routine vaccines sometimes or more often (36%), while about one-tenth reported discouraging vaccines (12%). These estimates include a small proportion of parents (6%) who reported both behaviors. Parents with higher confidence in the importance of vaccines for their child's health were more likely to be encouragers and less likely to be discouragers (both p < .05). Encouragers (n = 477) most often advocated for measles (50%) or seasonal flu vaccines (44%). These parents most often engaged family members and close friends, using strategies such as sharing vaccine experiences (47%), information about vaccine effectiveness (38%), and advice or opinions about vaccines (35%). Discouragers (n = 151) most often advocated against Covid-19 (53%) or seasonal flu vaccines (41%), with relatively few focusing on measles vaccine (10%). Discouragers engaged similar audiences and strategies as encouragers. CONCLUSION: Findings of our nationally representative study suggest that parents who encourage routine vaccination far outnumber those who discourage it. Given that encouragers often advocate for measles vaccine, future efforts to increase vaccination coverage should consider ways to partner with parent advocates to stem the alarming resurgence of this disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In 2025, the US recorded the highest number of measles cases in 25 years, signaling an urgent need for renewed attention to increasing childhood vaccination coverage. This study aimed to understand parents' role in advocating for and against childhood vaccines to identify future intervention partners.
Design and population
A nationally representative sample of 1,315 parents of children ages 9-12 years participated in an online survey about vaccine advocacy behaviors in 2025. The study focused on parents' behaviors regarding measles vaccine, which was the most frequently advocated for by encouragers.
Core methods
Separate weighted logistic regression models identified correlates of encouraging or discouraging other parents from getting routine vaccines for their children. The analysis included examining the strategies and audiences used by parents in their advocacy efforts.
Main results
Over one-third of parents reported encouraging routine vaccines sometimes or more often (36%), while about one-tenth reported discouraging vaccines (12%). These estimates include a small proportion of parents (6%) who reported both behaviors.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that parents who encourage routine vaccination far outnumber those who discourage it. The study highlights the importance of understanding the factors that influence parental vaccine advocacy behaviors.
Scientific and public-health significance
Given that encouragers often advocate for measles vaccine, future efforts to increase vaccination coverage should consider ways to partner with parent advocates to stem the alarming resurgence of this disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on self-reported data from parents, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it relies on survey responses rather than direct biological or clinical measurements.

2022年至2024年伊拉克巴比伦省儿童传染病趋势分析

Analysis of Communicable Disease Trends in Children: Babylon Governorate, Iraq from 2022 to 2024
作者： Sahar Hassan, Kareem Jebur Dhaidan
摘要中文翻译背景:传染病是对全球儿童健康的巨大挑战。本文提供了伊拉克希拉巴比伦省儿童中这些疾病的流行病学情况和模式,特别是膳食的重新出现。方法:对2022年1月1日至2024年12月31日期间7851份15岁以下儿童病历进行回顾性研究,采用巴比伦妇幼医院病历进行提取。使用描述性统计和推理检验对数据进行按年龄、性别和时间的趋势和分布分析。结果:飞沫和空气传播的感染方式占很大比例(89.1,n=6998)。最常见的是肺炎(59.3,n=4659),然后是 麻疹(21.0,n=1651)。麻疹 的急剧复苏被记录下来,2022 年为零病例,2023 年为 344 例,2024 年为 1,307 例。食源性感染 10.1% (n=795) 主要是甲型肝炎 (98.4% 食源性感染)。发现特定疾病的年龄和性别分布存在显着差异,具有统计学意义。年龄较小的儿童(岁)更多患有肺炎、百日咳和病毒性脑膜炎,而年龄较大的儿童(5-9 岁)则患有甲型肝炎。肺炎和病毒性脑膜炎以男性为主,而百日咳在女性中更为常见。结论:呼吸道传播疾病,特别是肺炎和麻疹是该地区儿童传染病负担最大的疾病。麻疹 的灾难性复活表明,麻疹 的免疫覆盖率和准备工作中存在严重差距。公共卫生领域的干预措施应重点加强常规和追赶的疫苗接种运动,改善呼吸道感染控制,改善针对高危人群的环境卫生和个人卫生措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传染病对全球儿童构成重大健康挑战,该研究旨在描述伊拉克巴比伦省儿童中这些疾病的流行病学模式和负担,重点关注 麻疹 的重新出现。
设计与对象
一项回顾性研究使用了巴比伦妇幼医院登记处 2022 年 1 月 1 日至 2024 年 12 月 31 日期间 7,851 份 15 岁以下儿童的医疗记录。
核心方法
从医院记录中提取数据,并使用描述性统计和推理测试进行分析,以评估按年龄、性别和时间划分的趋势和分布。
主要结果
麻疹 病例从 2022 年的零急剧增加到 2023 年的 344 例和 2024 年的 1,307 例,其中肺炎是最常见的疾病(59.3%,n=4,659),其次是 麻疹(21.0%,n=1,651)。
解释与创新
作者将 麻疹 的死灰复燃解释为该地区免疫覆盖率和 麻疹 准备工作方面存在严重差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,迫切需要加强常规和追赶的疫苗接种运动,改善呼吸道感染的控制,并加强高危年龄组的环境卫生和个人卫生措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、对单一医院登记的依赖以及缺乏对所报告疾病的详细病因学确认而受到限制,这可能会影响研究结果的准确性和普遍性。

Analysis of Communicable Disease Trends in Children: Babylon Governorate, Iraq from 2022 to 2024

Authors: Sahar Hassan, Kareem Jebur Dhaidan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Infectious diseases are a great challenge to the health of children globally. This paper provides an epidemiological scenario and patterns of these diseases among children in the Hilla Babylon Governorate, Iraq, and specifically, the re-emergence of meals. Methods: The retrospective study was carried out on 7851 medical records of children aged below 15 years between the period of January 1, 2022 and December 31, 2024. The registry of Babylon Maternity and Children Hospital was used to extract data. Data were analyzed in terms of trends and distributions by age, sex and time using descriptive statistics and inferential tests. Results: Droplet and airborne mode of infection were responsible of a huge percentage (89.1, n=6998). The most common one was pneumonia (59.3, n=4659), and then measles (21.0, n=1651). The dramatic resurgence of measles was recorded, and in 2022, there were zero cases, and in 2023, the rates were 344 and in 2024, 1,307. Foodborne infections 10.1% (n=795) were mainly hepatitis A (98.4% of foodborne infections). Significant differences in the age and sex distributions of particular diseases were found to be statistically significant. Younger children (years) had more pneumonia, pertussis, and viral meningitis whereas older children (5-9 years) had Hepatitis A. Pneumonia and viral meningitis had a high male predominance whereas pertussis was more prevalent in females. Conclusion: Respiratory-borne diseases and especially pneumonia and measles have the greatest burden of communicable disease among children in this region. The disastrous revival of measles points to the existence of severe gaps in immunization coverage and preparedness to the outbreaks. The interventions in the field of public health should be focused on the reinforcement of a routine and catch-up vaccination campaign, the improvement of respiratory infections control, and the improvement of sanitation and hygiene measures directed at high-risk age groups.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Infectious diseases pose a major health challenge for children globally, and the study aimed to characterize the epidemiological patterns and burden of these diseases among children in Babylon Governorate, Iraq, with a focus on the re-emergence of measles.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted using 7,851 medical records of children aged below 15 years from the Babylon Maternity and Children Hospital registry between January 1, 2022, and December 31, 2024.
Core methods
Data were extracted from hospital records and analyzed using descriptive statistics and inferential tests to assess trends and distributions by age, sex, and time.
Main results
Measles cases dramatically resurged from zero in 2022 to 344 in 2023 and 1,307 in 2024, with pneumonia being the most common disease (59.3%, n=4,659) followed by measles (21.0%, n=1,651).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the resurgence of measles as indicating severe gaps in immunization coverage and outbreak preparedness in the region.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the urgent need to strengthen routine and catch-up vaccination campaigns, improve control of respiratory infections, and enhance sanitation and hygiene measures for high-risk age groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, reliance on a single hospital registry, and lack of detailed etiological confirmation for reported diseases, which may affect the accuracy and generalizability of the findings.

准备好还是面临风险?在儿科实体器官移植前评估活病毒 疫苗 覆盖率和 疫苗 免疫原性

Prepared or at-risk? Evaluating live viral vaccine coverage and vaccine immunogenicity before pediatric solid organ transplantation
作者： Benhur Sirvan Cetin, Caitlin Brammer, William Otto, Kerrigan Perkins, Teresa Ambrosino, Hilary Miller-Handley, Mark Murphy, Grant Paulsen, Emily Cain, Kathleen Campbell
摘要中文翻译儿童实体器官移植受者发生 疫苗 可预防疾病并发症的风险很高,但移植前 疫苗 的覆盖范围往往不一致。我们对 2018 年至 2024 年间 421 名儿科移植受者进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,以评估 疫苗接种 在初始评估、上市和移植时对水痘和 麻疹 的状态和血清学免疫。在移植时,19.2%的水痘患者和21.6%的麻疹患者未接种疫苗,其中婴儿的麻疹不完全接种率最高。部分患者(水痘患者为 7.1%,麻疹 患者为 8.6%)在移植前从完全 麻疹 状态转变为不完全 疫苗接种 状态,这一变化与从评估到列入名单的时间较短有关。肾脏受者的 疫苗接种 完成率最高 (>94%),而肝脏和心脏受者的完成率则低得多 (58%–72%)。在完全接种疫苗的患者中,观察到水痘 (23.6%) 和 麻疹 (13.0%) 血清阴性。总之,许多儿科移植候选人,特别是婴儿和等待肝脏或心脏移植的患者,都没有完全接种疫苗。疫苗接种 状态从评估到移植的动态变化凸显了迫切需要持续监测和改进策略来保护这些弱势儿童。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
儿童实体器官移植受者发生 疫苗 可预防疾病并发症的风险很高,但移植前 疫苗 的覆盖范围往往不一致。本研究旨在评估这些患者的 麻疹 状态以及 麻疹 和水痘的血清学免疫。
设计与对象
对 2018 年至 2024 年间 421 名儿科移植受者进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,以评估 疫苗接种 在初始评估、上市和移植时对水痘和 麻疹 的状态和血清学免疫。
核心方法
该研究通过回顾性数据收集和血清学检测评估了水痘和 麻疹 的 疫苗接种 状态和血清学免疫。它评估了从评估到移植期间 疫苗接种 状态的变化,并将这些变化与评估和列出之间的时间间隔联系起来。
主要结果
在移植时,21.6% 的 麻疹 患者疫苗接种不足,其中婴儿的 麻疹 不完全接种率最高。一部分患者(麻疹 为 8.6%)在移植前从完全 麻疹 状态转变为不完全 疫苗接种 状态,这一变化与从评估到列出的时间较短有关。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 麻疹 移植前 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的不一致以及 疫苗接种 状态变化的动态性质,特别是在婴儿和等待肝脏或心脏移植的患者中。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调需要改进 疫苗接种 策略和持续监测,以保护儿科移植候选者免受 疫苗 可预防疾病(如 麻疹)的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的数据缺失而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于观察数据而不是实验或前瞻性方法。

Prepared or at-risk? Evaluating live viral vaccine coverage and vaccine immunogenicity before pediatric solid organ transplantation

Authors: Benhur Sirvan Cetin, Caitlin Brammer, William Otto, Kerrigan Perkins, Teresa Ambrosino, Hilary Miller-Handley, Mark Murphy, Grant Paulsen, Emily Cain, Kathleen Campbell
Original abstract (English)
Pediatric solid organ transplant recipients are at high risk for complications from vaccine-preventable diseases, yet pre-transplant vaccination coverage is often inconsistent. We conducted a retrospective cohort study of 421 pediatric transplant recipients between 2018 and 2024 to evaluate vaccination status and serologic immunity for varicella and measles at the time of initial evaluation, listing, and transplantation. At the time of transplant, 19.2% of patients for varicella and 21.6% for measles were under-vaccinated, with infants having the highest rates of incomplete vaccination. A portion of patients (7.1% for varicella, 8.6% for measles) transitioned from full to incomplete vaccination status during the pre-transplant period, a change associated with a shorter time from evaluation to listing. Kidney recipients had the highest completed vaccination rates (>94%), whereas rates for liver and heart recipients were substantially lower (58%–72%). Among fully vaccinated patients, seronegativity was observed for varicella (23.6%) and measles (13.0%). In conclusion, many pediatric transplant candidates, particularly infants and those awaiting liver or heart transplant, are incompletely vaccinated. A dynamic change in vaccination status from evaluation to transplant highlights an urgent need for continuous monitoring and improved strategies to protect these vulnerable children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pediatric solid organ transplant recipients are at high risk for complications from vaccine-preventable diseases, yet pre-transplant vaccination coverage is often inconsistent. This study aimed to evaluate vaccination status and serologic immunity for measles and varicella in these patients.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study of 421 pediatric transplant recipients between 2018 and 2024 was conducted to evaluate vaccination status and serologic immunity for varicella and measles at the time of initial evaluation, listing, and transplantation.
Core methods
The study evaluated vaccination status and serologic immunity for varicella and measles through retrospective data collection and serologic testing. It assessed changes in vaccination status from evaluation to transplant and linked these changes to the time interval between evaluation and listing.
Main results
At the time of transplant, 21.6% of patients for measles were under-vaccinated, with infants having the highest rates of incomplete vaccination. A portion of patients (8.6% for measles) transitioned from full to incomplete vaccination status during the pre-transplant period, a change associated with a shorter time from evaluation to listing.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the inconsistency in pre-transplant vaccination coverage for measles and the dynamic nature of vaccination status changes, particularly in infants and those awaiting liver or heart transplant.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the need for improved vaccination strategies and continuous monitoring to protect pediatric transplant candidates from vaccine-preventable diseases like measles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for missing data. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on observational data rather than experimental or prospective methods.

疫苗接种 和感染后人类 B 细胞记忆的组织定向维持 2260241

Tissue-directed maintenance of human B-cell memory after vaccination and infection 2260241
作者： Eric Zhang, Alex B George, Tatchapon Ajavavarakula, Matteo Porotto, Peter A. Sims, Donna Farber
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 疫苗 的保护功效和持久性各不相同。临床滴度研究表明,一些,例如麻疹 赋予终生免疫力,而其他免疫力则在数月或数年内减弱。维持这些反应的记忆 B 细胞 (MBC) 主要存在于淋巴组织中,但目前尚不清楚这些区室是否维持不同的子集、它们如何促进 疫苗 免疫、以及它们如何随年龄或抗原而变化。方法 我们通过流式细胞术和配对单细胞 RNA 和 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 测序,对配对血液、脾脏、淋巴结和骨髓中的四种 Ag 特异性 MBC 群体进行了分析,这些群体针对儿童 疫苗 麻疹 和破伤风以及普遍存在的病毒 SARS-CoV-2 和 H1N1 流感。结果 MBC 同种型、亚型和表型在组织部位、特异性和年龄之间存在显着差异。淋巴结为 储存宿主,代表经典 IgG+ 记忆,高表达驻留标记 CD69。在其他位点,虽然 麻疹 保留了经典表型,但破伤风 MBC 的非转换记忆得到了丰富。值得注意的是,SARS-CoV-2 和流感反应高度不同,但随着年龄的增长,血液、骨髓和脾脏中独特地积累了低反应性 CD27-CD21-非典型记忆。RNA测序解析了在不同组织中富集的三个核心MBC亚群:淋巴结中的组织适应经典MBC、脾中的边缘区样MBC和骨髓中的非典型MBC。所有三个亚群都代表抗原特异性群体,但它们的相对比例由组织和抗原决定。克隆和系统发育分析表明,扩大的 Ag 特异性克隆(包括具有相同 BCR 的细胞)跨越多个子集,这表明 MBC 身份是灵活的,但主要由局部环境而不是谱系决定。结论 这项工作揭示了 疫苗 的功能上不同的组织 储存宿主 和感染诱导的记忆以及 宿主 和塑造它们的抗原因子,突出了 疫苗 设计的新机会。资金来源 NIH U19 AI128949、DARPA 主题类别 淋巴细胞分化和外周维护 (LYM)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解记忆 B 细胞 (MBC) 在 疫苗接种 和感染后如何在不同组织中维持,特别关注淋巴组织中是否存在不同的亚群,以及它们如何促进 疫苗 免疫,随年龄变化或因抗原而异。
设计与对象
该研究分析了配对血液、脾脏、淋巴结和骨髓样本中儿童 疫苗(麻疹 和破伤风)和普遍存在的病毒(SARS-CoV-2 和 H1N1 流感)的抗原特异性 MBC 群体。
核心方法
研究人员使用流式细胞术和配对单细胞 RNA 和 B 细胞受体 (BCR) 测序来分析 MBC 群体,其中 RNA 测序解析了不同组织中富集的三个核心 MBC 亚群。
主要结果
MBC 同种型、亚型和表型在组织部位、特异性和年龄之间存在显着差异;淋巴结为 储存宿主,代表经典 IgG+ 记忆,CD69 高表达;扩展的抗原特异性克隆跨越多个子集,表明由当地环境决定的灵活的 MBC 身份。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为揭示了 疫苗 的功能上不同的组织 储存宿主 和感染诱导的记忆,由 宿主 和抗原因子塑造,突出了 疫苗 设计的新机会。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过揭示组织特异性记忆 B 细胞维持如何随抗原和年龄而变化,为 疫苗 设计提供信息,从而可能指导增强持久免疫力的策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度很高,因为它使用高级测序全面分析了多种组织和抗原,但其局限性包括超出研究人群和抗原的潜在普遍性。

Tissue-directed maintenance of human B-cell memory after vaccination and infection 2260241

Authors: Eric Zhang, Alex B George, Tatchapon Ajavavarakula, Matteo Porotto, Peter A. Sims, Donna Farber
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Vaccines differ in their efficacy and durability of protection. Clinical titer studies show that some, e.g. measles, confer lifelong immunity, while others wane within months or years. The memory B cells (MBCs) that sustain these responses reside mainly in lymphoid tissues, but it remains unclear whether these compartments maintain distinct subsets, how they contribute to vaccine immunity, and how they vary with age or antigen. Methods We profiled four ag-specific MBC populations, specific for childhood vaccines measles and tetanus and ubiquitous viruses SARS-CoV-2 and H1N1 influenza, across paired blood, spleen, lymph nodes, and bone marrow, by flow cytometry and paired single-cell RNA and B-cell receptor (BCR) sequencing. Results MBC isotype, subset, and phenotype differed markedly between tissue sites, specificity, and age. Lymph nodes were reservoirs for classical IgG+ memory with high expression of the residency marker CD69. In other sites, whereas measles retained a classical phenotype, tetanus MBCs were enriched for non-switched memory. Notably, SARS-CoV-2 and influenza responses were highly switched but, with age, uniquely accumulated hyporesponsive CD27- CD21- atypical memory in the blood, bone marrow, and spleen. RNA sequencing resolved three core MBC subsets enriched in distinct tissues: tissue-adapted classical MBCs in lymph nodes, marginal zone—like MBCs in spleen, and atypical MBCs in bone marrow. All three subsets were represented within ag-specific populations, but their relative proportions were shaped by both tissue and antigen. Clonal and phylogenetic analyses revealed that expanded ag-specific clones, including cells with identical BCRs, spanned multiple subsets, indicating that MBC identity is flexible but primarily shaped by local context rather than lineage. Conclusion This work uncovers functionally distinct tissue reservoirs of vaccine and infection-induced memory and the host and antigen factors that shape them, highlighting new opportunities for vaccine design. Funding Source NIH U19 AI128949, DARPA Topic Categories Lymphocyte Differentiation and Peripheral Maintenance (LYM)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how memory B cells (MBCs) are maintained in different tissues after vaccination and infection, particularly focusing on whether distinct subsets exist in lymphoid tissues and how they contribute to vaccine immunity, vary with age, or differ by antigen.
Design and population
The study profiled antigen-specific MBC populations for childhood vaccines (measles and tetanus) and ubiquitous viruses (SARS-CoV-2 and H1N1 influenza) across paired blood, spleen, lymph nodes, and bone marrow samples.
Core methods
Researchers used flow cytometry and paired single-cell RNA and B-cell receptor (BCR) sequencing to analyze MBC populations, with RNA sequencing resolving three core MBC subsets enriched in distinct tissues.
Main results
MBC isotype, subset, and phenotype differed markedly between tissue sites, specificity, and age; lymph nodes were reservoirs for classical IgG+ memory with high CD69 expression; and expanded antigen-specific clones spanned multiple subsets, indicating flexible MBC identity shaped by local context.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as uncovering functionally distinct tissue reservoirs of vaccine and infection-induced memory, shaped by host and antigen factors, highlighting new opportunities for vaccine design.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform vaccine design by revealing how tissue-specific memory B cell maintenance varies by antigen and age, potentially guiding strategies to enhance durable immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is high, as it comprehensively profiled multiple tissues and antigens using advanced sequencing, though limitations include potential generalizability beyond the studied populations and antigens.

用于针对尼帕病毒感染的靶向治疗的免疫原性肽-配体缀合物的计算设计

Computational design of immunogenic peptide–ligand conjugates for targeted therapy against Nipah virus infection
作者： Sudip Prasad Jena, Prateek Nayak, M. A. Adithyan, Manoviraj Morey, R. Vihaas Tharsini, Abilash Valsala Gopalakrishnan, Kuntal Pal, Sabina Evan Prince
摘要中文翻译简介 尼帕病毒 (NiV) 由于其高传染性和相关的 死亡率 发病率,对全球公共卫生构成重大风险。目前,尚无获得许可的 疫苗 或 抗病毒药物 药物可用于治疗 NiV 感染,因此临床管理仅限于支持性护理。负责将 NiV 与 宿主 细胞受体 (ephrin-B2/B3) 结合的病毒受体糖蛋白代表了理想的治疗靶点。这项研究提出了一种新型肽-配体缀合物 (PLC) 免疫治疗方法,该方法利用 NiV 流行人群中预先存在的 免疫应答 来选择性地靶向和消除受感染的细胞。方法我们采用生物分子建模(计算机)来建立结合亲和力,并使用从 MolProphet 数据库获得的化合物库进行对接研究。选择不可裂解的肟接头来增强物理稳定性并确保配体和肽组分之间的牢固结合。该肽经过工程改造,含有源自 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 疫苗 的免疫原性最小表位区域,这些表位区域是根据居住在 NiV 流行地区的个体的高免疫率和长效记忆反应而选择的。结果 PLC 设计证明了对 NiV 感染细胞上存在的跨膜蛋白的选择性结合能力。肟接头提供了增强的稳定性,肽表位设计成功地整合了与已建立的长期免疫相关的区域。讨论 该 PLC 系统通过利用预先存在的免疫识别来促进 NiV 感染细胞的选择性清除,为 抗病毒药物 治疗开发提供了一个有前途的框架。研究结果强调了 PLC 在治疗开发中的关键结构成分和功能作用,包括药物靶点筛选和增强靶向特异性和分子稳定性的合理设计策略。未来的工作应侧重于计算预测的实验验证和体外/体内功效研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一种新型免疫治疗方法来对抗尼帕病毒感染,因为它的高传染性和相关的 死亡率 率对全球公共卫生构成重大风险。
设计与对象
该研究采用计算方法设计肽-配体缀合物(PLC),用于针对尼帕病毒感染的靶向治疗,重点关注可以利用预先存在的 免疫应答 的 NiV 流行人群。
核心方法
研究人员使用生物分子模型(计算机)来建立结合亲和力,并与 MolProphet 数据库中的化合物库进行对接研究。他们还对肽进行了改造,使其包含来自 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 疫苗 的免疫原性表位,这些表位是根据其高免疫率和长效记忆反应而选择的。
主要结果
PLC 设计证明了对尼帕病毒感染细胞上存在的跨膜蛋白的选择性结合能力。
解释与创新
该研究将这些发现解释为 抗病毒药物 治疗开发的一个有前途的框架,利用预先存在的免疫识别来促进尼帕病毒感染细胞的选择性清除。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 PLC 在治疗开发中的结构成分和功能作用提供了重要的见解,这可以为未来增强 抗病毒药物 治疗 的靶向特异性和分子稳定性的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算设计,缺乏实验验证,这使得证据强度降至中等。

Computational design of immunogenic peptide–ligand conjugates for targeted therapy against Nipah virus infection

Authors: Sudip Prasad Jena, Prateek Nayak, M. A. Adithyan, Manoviraj Morey, R. Vihaas Tharsini, Abilash Valsala Gopalakrishnan, Kuntal Pal, Sabina Evan Prince
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Nipah virus (NiV) poses a major risk to global public health due to its high infectivity and associated mortality rates. Currently, no licensed vaccines or antiviral medications are available for NiV infection, leaving clinical management limited to supportive care. The viral receptor glycoprotein responsible for binding NiV to host cell receptors (ephrin-B2/B3) represents an ideal therapeutic target. This study proposes a novel peptide-ligand conjugate (PLC) immunotherapeutic approach that exploits pre-existing immune responses in NiV-endemic populations to selectively target and eliminate infected cells. Methods We employed biomolecular modeling ( in silico ) to establish binding affinities and perform docking studies using a compound library obtained from the MolProphet database. A non-cleavable oxime linker was selected to enhance physical stability and ensure robust conjugation between ligand and peptide components. The peptide was engineered to contain immunogenic minimal epitope regions derived from measles, mumps, and rubella vaccines, selected based on their high immunization rates and long-lived memory responses in individuals residing in NiV-endemic areas. Results The PLC design demonstrated selective binding capacity to a transmembrane protein present on NiV-infected cells. The oxime linker provided enhanced stability, and the peptide epitope design successfully incorporated regions associated with established long-term immunity. Discussion This PLC system represents a promising framework for antiviral therapeutic development by harnessing pre-existing immune recognition to promote selective clearance of NiV-infected cells. The findings highlight critical structural components and functional roles of PLCs in therapeutic development, including drug target screening and rational design strategies for enhancing targeting specificity and molecular stability. Future work should focus on experimental validation of the computational predictions and in vitro / in vivo efficacy studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a novel immunotherapeutic approach to combat Nipah virus infection, as it poses a major risk to global public health due to its high infectivity and associated mortality rates.
Design and population
The study employed computational methods to design peptide–ligand conjugates (PLCs) for targeted therapy against Nipah virus infection, focusing on NiV-endemic populations where pre-existing immune responses could be leveraged.
Core methods
The researchers used biomolecular modeling (in silico) to establish binding affinities and perform docking studies with a compound library from the MolProphet database. They also engineered the peptide to include immunogenic epitopes from measles, mumps, and rubella vaccines, selected based on their high immunization rates and long-lived memory responses.
Main results
The PLC design demonstrated selective binding capacity to a transmembrane protein present on Nipah virus-infected cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets the findings as a promising framework for antiviral therapeutic development, leveraging pre-existing immune recognition to promote selective clearance of Nipah virus-infected cells.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide critical insights into the structural components and functional roles of PLCs in therapeutic development, which could inform future strategies for enhancing targeting specificity and molecular stability in antiviral treatments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to computational design and lacks experimental validation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

疫苗 误解和医护人员 2306364

Vaccine Misconceptions and Healthcare Workers 2306364
作者： Rachael Lister, Katrina Etts, Lisa Cuchara
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 尽管 疫苗接种 是最有效的公共卫生干预措施之一,但 疫苗 的信心最近有所下降。广泛传播的错误信息和对公共卫生机构信任度的降低给医疗保健行业带来了挑战。联邦沟通中的感知差距使临床和教育信息寻求进一步复杂化。由于机构可信度受到质疑,医疗保健专业人员依赖分散的在线资源,这放大了不确定性和错误信息。民意调查数据显示,家长们表示信任非医学影响者(即 Joe Rogan、RFK Jr. 和儿童保护基金会)获取重要的儿童健康信息。科学的政治化凸显了加强临床培训和医疗保健专业人员的免疫学和 疫苗 素养教育的迫切需要。方法 我们对医疗保健/科学 (HCW/S) 和非医疗保健工作者 (NHCW) 的从业人员进行了 IRB 批准的匿名评估 (n = 7711)。结果 结果显示,虽然 HCW/S 通常比 NHCW 持有更准确的 疫苗 认知,但误解仍然很常见。对于“有证据表明 麻疹 疫苗 中的汞可导致自闭症”这一声明,HCW 的加权平均得分较低(1.97 vs. 2.35;n = 7711;p = 0.05)。然而,32% 的人仍然选择强烈同意、同意或既不同意也不不同意,尽管这一说法实际上不正确。在其他声明中也观察到了类似的分析,其中 33% 的医护人员认为流感 疫苗 可以引起流感。结论 在传统公共卫生当局信任度下降的情况下,植根于核心免疫学概念和沟通素养的 疫苗 教育至关重要。加强免疫学课程、CME、CEU、角色扮演、讨论等对于确保医疗保健专业人员能够批判性地评估证据、识别可靠来源并反驳错误信息以维持一支具有科学知识的员工队伍至关重要。资金来源 不适用 主题类别 免疫学教育和传播 (EDU)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 疫苗接种 是最有效的公共卫生干预措施之一,但 疫苗 的信心最近有所下降。广泛传播的错误信息和对公共卫生机构信任度的降低给医疗保健行业带来了挑战。
设计与对象
我们对医疗保健/科学 (HCW/S) 和非医疗保健工作者 (NHCW) 的从业人员进行了 IRB 批准的匿名评估 (n = 7711)。
核心方法
该研究对医护人员和非医护人员进行了匿名评估,以评估他们对 疫苗 相关陈述的看法。
主要结果
结果显示,尽管 HCW/S 通常比 NHCW 持有更准确的 疫苗 认知,但误解仍然很常见。对于“有证据表明 麻疹 疫苗 中的汞可导致自闭症”这一声明,HCW 的加权平均得分较低(1.97 vs. 2.35;n = 7711;p = 0.05)。
解释与创新
科学的政治化凸显了加强临床培训和医疗保健专业人员的免疫学和 疫苗 素养教育的迫切需要。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或证据强度评估的具体局限性。

Vaccine Misconceptions and Healthcare Workers 2306364

Authors: Rachael Lister, Katrina Etts, Lisa Cuchara
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Despite vaccination being one of the most effective public-health interventions, vaccine confidence has recently declined. Widespread misinformation and reduced trust in public health agencies have created challenges for those in healthcare. Perceived gaps in federal communication further complicate clinical and educational information-seeking. As institutional credibility is questioned, healthcare professionals rely on fragmented online sources that amplify uncertainty and misinformation. Polling data show that parents report trusting non-medical influencers (i.e. Joe Rogan, RFK Jr., and the Children’s Defense Fund) for critical children’s health information. The politicization of science underscores the urgent need to strengthen immunology and vaccine literacy education across clinical training and healthcare professionals. Methods We conducted an IRB-approved, anonymous assessment (n = 7711) across those employed in healthcare/science (HCW/S) and non-healthcare workers (NHCW). Results Results showed that although HCW/S generally held more accurate vaccine perceptions than NHCW, misconceptions remained common. For the statement, “There is evidence that the mercury in the measles vaccine can cause autism”, HCW had a lower weighted mean score (1.97 vs. 2.35; n = 7711; p = 0.05). However, 32% still selected strongly agree, agree, or neither agree nor disagree, despite this statement being factually incorrect. Similar analyses were observed across other statements, including 33% of HCW believing the influenza vaccine can cause influenza. Conclusion Vaccine education rooted in core immunologic concepts and communication literacy is critical amid declining trust in traditional public-health authorities. Strengthening immunology curricula, CMEs, CEUs, role playing, discussions, etc. are critical to ensure that healthcare professionals can critically evaluate evidence, identify reliable sources, and counter misinformation to sustain a scientifically informed workforce. Funding Source n/a Topic Categories Immunology Education and Communication (EDU)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite vaccination being one of the most effective public-health interventions, vaccine confidence has recently declined. Widespread misinformation and reduced trust in public health agencies have created challenges for those in healthcare.
Design and population
We conducted an IRB-approved, anonymous assessment (n = 7711) across those employed in healthcare/science (HCW/S) and non-healthcare workers (NHCW).
Core methods
The study involved an anonymous assessment of healthcare workers and non-healthcare workers to evaluate their perceptions of vaccine-related statements.
Main results
Results showed that although HCW/S generally held more accurate vaccine perceptions than NHCW, misconceptions remained common. For the statement, “There is evidence that the mercury in the measles vaccine can cause autism”, HCW had a lower weighted mean score (1.97 vs. 2.35; n = 7711; p = 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The politicization of science underscores the urgent need to strengthen immunology and vaccine literacy education across clinical training and healthcare professionals.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening immunology curricula, CMEs, CEUs, role playing, discussions, etc., are critical to ensure that healthcare professionals can critically evaluate evidence, identify reliable sources, and counter misinformation to sustain a scientifically informed workforce.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

文化转型与文化转型

Karakterisasi transformasi kultur sel ginjal monyet ekor panjang secara molekuler
作者： Gita Iftitah Renitia, Sela Septima Mariya, Iin Indriawati, Alysa Kamira Kurniani Tirta, Ellis Dwi Ayuningsih, Uus Saepuloh, Adi Winarto, Wahono Esthi Prasetyaningtyas, Rifani Rifani, Huda Shalahudin Darusman
摘要中文翻译Satwa primata memiliki peran penting dalam penelitian Biomedis karena memiliki kemiripan 遗传学和 fisiologis yang erat dengan manusia。Sel ginjal yang ditransformasi bersumber dari monyet ekor panjang (Mf) asal Indonesia telah berhasil dikembangkan untuk tujuan menjadi sel model in vivo dan pengembangan raiset vaksin.请注意不要将其损坏。Karakterisasi sel ginjal conversionasi Mf belum pernah dilakukan。Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk karakterisasi molecularkuler sel ginjal Mf yang dilakukantransasidengan mengkarakterisasi keberadaan gen receptorvirus,gen penekantum,dan gen proliferasi.分析 CD46 和 CD155 受体病毒 麻疹 和脊髓灰质炎、内皮唾液酸蛋白基因、CD24、vWF 细胞形态学 (成纤维细胞、上皮细胞、内膜细胞)、serta 基因 PCNA 和 P53 细胞增殖和 Penekan 肿瘤。PCR 技术分析。Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa gen target dalam Studi ini (CD46、CD155、CD24、内皮唾液酸蛋白、vWF、PCNA、dan P53) berhasil teridentifikasi dalam sel ginjal Mf 转化、sel Vero 和 sel ginjal Mf 正常。在此过程中,分子生物学研究人员将其转化为分子生物学研究,并在正常和贝尔波滕西模型中进行体外实验。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了生物医学研究(特别是 疫苗 开发)对源自非人类灵长类动物的体外细胞模型的需求,这些模型与人类细胞非常相似。它强调了印度尼西亚长尾猕猴(Macaca fasciculis)转化肾细胞分子特征方面的差距。
设计与对象
该研究利用分子技术对长尾猕猴 (Mf) 的转化肾细胞进行了表征。该研究的重点是来自印度尼西亚猕猴的细胞,并将转化细胞与正常肾细胞和 Vero 细胞进行比较。
核心方法
使用 PCR 分析靶基因的存在,以检测 麻疹病毒 受体(CD46 和 CD155)、细胞形态标记物(内皮唾液酸蛋白、CD24 和 vWF)以及增殖和肿瘤抑制标记物(PCNA 和 P53)。
主要结果
在转化的 Mf 肾细胞、正常 Mf 肾细胞和 Vero 细胞中成功鉴定了所有靶基因(CD46、CD155、CD24、内皮唾液酸蛋白、vWF、PCNA 和 P53)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明转化的 Mf 肾细胞保留了与正常 Mf 肾细胞相似的分子特征,支持它们作为生物医学研究和 疫苗 开发的体外模型的潜在用途。以前没有进行过这种表征。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,转化的 Mf 肾细胞可以作为 麻疹病毒 研究和 疫苗 开发的可靠体外模型,有可能改善临床前测试并减少对人源细胞的依赖。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于通过 PCR 进行的分子表征,不包括功能测定或直接的 麻疹病毒 感染研究,这削弱了结论的强度。该研究仅基于抽象级别的证据,进一步降低了可信度。

Karakterisasi transformasi kultur sel ginjal monyet ekor panjang secara molekuler

Authors: Gita Iftitah Renitia, Sela Septima Mariya, Iin Indriawati, Alysa Kamira Kurniani Tirta, Ellis Dwi Ayuningsih, Uus Saepuloh, Adi Winarto, Wahono Esthi Prasetyaningtyas, Rifani Rifani, Huda Shalahudin Darusman
Original abstract (English)
Satwa primata memiliki peran penting dalam penelitian biomedis karena memiliki kemiripan genetik dan fisiologis yang erat dengan manusia. Sel ginjal yang ditransformasi bersumber dari monyet ekor panjang (Mf) asal Indonesia telah berhasil dikembangkan untuk tujuan menjadi sel model in vitro dan pengembangan riset vaksin. Sel lestari dengan kualitas yang baik harus memiliki karakteristik yang sama dengan sel induknya. Karakterisasi sel ginjal transformasi Mf belum pernah dilakukan. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk karakterisasi molekuler sel ginjal Mf yang dilakukan transformasi dengan mengkarakterisasi keberadaan gen reseptor virus, gen penekan tumor, dan gen proliferasi. Analisis dilakukan terhadap gen CD46 dan CD155 sebagai reseptor virus measles dan polio, gen endosialin, CD24, dan vWF sebagai penanda morfologi sel (fibroblast, epitel, dan endotel), serta gen PCNA dan P53 sebagai penanda proliferasi dan penekan tumor. Keberadaan gen dianalisis melalui teknik PCR. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa gen target dalam studi ini (CD46, CD155, CD24, endosialin, vWF, PCNA, dan P53) berhasil teridentifikasi dalam sel ginjal Mf transformasi, sel Vero dan sel ginjal Mf normal. Temuan ini menunjukkan bahwa secara molekuler sel ginjal Mf yang ditransformasi menunjukkan karakteristik secara molekuler yang sama dengan sel ginjal Mf normal dan berpotensi sebagai model sel untuk penelitian biomedis sebagai sediaan in vitro untuk pengembangan vaksin.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for in vitro cell models derived from non-human primates that closely resemble human cells for biomedical research, particularly vaccine development. It highlights the gap in molecular characterization of transformed kidney cells from long-tailed macaques (Macaca fascicularis) from Indonesia.
Design and population
The research characterized transformed kidney cells from long-tailed macaques (Mf) using molecular techniques. The study focused on cells sourced from Indonesian macaques and compared transformed cells with normal kidney cells and Vero cells.
Core methods
The presence of target genes was analyzed using PCR to detect measles virus receptors (CD46 and CD155), cell morphology markers (endosialin, CD24, and vWF), and proliferation and tumor suppressor markers (PCNA and P53).
Main results
All target genes (CD46, CD155, CD24, endosialin, vWF, PCNA, and P53) were successfully identified in the transformed Mf kidney cells, normal Mf kidney cells, and Vero cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that transformed Mf kidney cells retain molecular characteristics similar to normal Mf kidney cells, supporting their potential use as in vitro models for biomedical research and vaccine development. This characterization had not been previously performed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that transformed Mf kidney cells could serve as reliable in vitro models for measles virus research and vaccine development, potentially improving preclinical testing and reducing reliance on human-derived cells.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to molecular characterization via PCR and does not include functional assays or direct measles virus infection studies, which weakens the strength of the conclusions. The study is based solely on abstract-level evidence, further reducing confidence.

使用混合卷积神经网络的皮肤疾病分类

Skin Disease Classification Using Hybrid Convolutional Neural Network
作者： Alfito Putra Fajar Pratama, Hasih Pratiwi, Isnandar Slamet
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在使用混合卷积神经网络(CNN)方法对皮肤疾病进行分类。混合CNN是CNN模型和机器学习方法之间的集成。使用的分类方法包括逻辑回归、随机森林和XGBoost。经过数据增强过程后,使用 ResNet 作为特征提取和逻辑回归作为分类方法时获得了最高性能结果,准确率为 82%,宏观 f1 分数为 80%,在皮肤病检测中表现出高性能。混合CNN与机器学习方法显示出皮肤病图像特征提取的能力并达到最佳性能。https://doi.org/10.3390/s22020587 - Wang,Y.,Pan,Z.,Zheng,J.,Qian,L.,Li,M.:脉冲星候选分类的混合集成方法。摘要 皮肤病有多种原因,包括接触化学物质、阳光、病毒、细菌和其他因素。皮肤病的分类至关重要,因为它们的症状相似,例如红疹、瘙痒和烧灼感。这些疾病的症状相似,很难通过视觉区分。使用的数据集是MSLD v2.0,是6类皮肤病图像数据,分别是猴痘、牛痘、水痘、麻疹、手足口病、健康。本研究使用三种CNN模型,即EfficientNet、DenseNet和ResNet作为特征提取。数据增强也用于增加数据变化。访问本章 税费计算将在结帐时完成 购买仅供个人使用 参考 - Srisantyorini,T.,Cahyaningsih,N.F.: Analisis kejadian penyakit kulit pada pemulung di tempat pengolahan sampah terpadu (TPST) kelurahan sumur batu kecamatan bantar gebang kota bek...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用混合卷积神经网络(CNN)方法对皮肤病进行分类,因为各种皮肤病的症状相似,导致视觉区分困难。
设计与对象
本研究使用MSLD v2.0数据集,其中包含6类皮肤病图像:猴痘、牛痘、水痘、麻疹、手足口病和健康。
核心方法
混合 CNN 集成了三种 CNN 模型(EfficientNet、DenseNet 和 ResNet)进行特征提取,并与机器学习方法(逻辑回归、随机森林和 XGBoost)相结合进行分类,以及数据增强以增加数据变化。
主要结果
使用 ResNet 进行特征提取和逻辑回归进行分类实现了最高性能,数据增强后的准确率为 82%,宏观 f1 分数为 80%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,混合CNN与机器学习方法有效地从皮肤病图像中提取特征,并取得了最佳性能,展示了皮肤病检测的高性能。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为皮肤病自动化诊断工具的开发提供信息,从而有可能改善临床环境中的早期检测和分类。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它依赖于单一数据集,并且没有解决对其他人群或现实临床环境的潜在普遍性。

Skin Disease Classification Using Hybrid Convolutional Neural Network

Authors: Alfito Putra Fajar Pratama, Hasih Pratiwi, Isnandar Slamet
Original abstract (English)
This research aims to classify skin diseases using a hybrid Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) method. Hybrid CNN is an integration between the CNN model and machine learning methods. The classification methods used include logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost. The highest performance results are obtained when using ResNet as a feature extraction and logistic regression as a classification method after going through the data augmentation process with an accuracy of 82% and a macro f1-score of 80%, which showed high performance in skin disease detection. Hybrid CNN with machine learning method shows the ability to feature extraction of skin disease images and achieves optimal performance. https://doi.org/10.3390/s22020587 - Wang, Y., Pan, Z., Zheng, J., Qian, L., Li, M.: A hybrid ensemble method for pulsar candidate classification. Abstract Skin diseases have a variety of causes, including exposure to chemicals, sunlight, viruses, bacteria, and other factors. Classification of skin diseases is essential because of their similar symptoms, such as red rashes, itching, and burning. The similar symptoms of the diseases make it difficult to distinguish them visually. The dataset used is MSLD v2.0, which is skin disease image data with 6 classes, namely monkeypox, cowpox, chickenpox, measles, HFMD, and healthy. This research uses three CNN models, namely EfficientNet, DenseNet, and ResNet, as feature extraction. Data augmentation is also applied to increase data variation. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to classify skin diseases using a hybrid Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) method due to the similar symptoms of various skin diseases, which make visual distinction difficult.
Design and population
This research used the MSLD v2.0 dataset, which contains skin disease images with 6 classes: monkeypox, cowpox, chickenpox, measles, HFMD, and healthy.
Core methods
The hybrid CNN integrated three CNN models (EfficientNet, DenseNet, and ResNet) for feature extraction and combined them with machine learning methods (logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost) for classification, along with data augmentation to increase data variation.
Main results
The highest performance was achieved using ResNet for feature extraction and logistic regression for classification, with an accuracy of 82% and a macro f1-score of 80% after data augmentation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the hybrid CNN with machine learning methods effectively extracted features from skin disease images and achieved optimal performance, demonstrating high capability in skin disease detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of automated diagnostic tools for skin diseases, potentially improving early detection and classification in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate, as it relies on a single dataset and does not address potential generalizability to other populations or real-world clinical environments.

亚急性硬化性全脑炎 中的长期生存(≥2 年):对长期生存者的系统评价。

Long-term survival (≥2 years) in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: a systematic review of prolonged survivors.
作者： Ravindra Kumar Garg, Shweta Pandey, Imran Rizvi, Sanjay Singhal
摘要中文翻译目的:亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 是一种进行性且通常致命的疾病,生存率差异很大。本系统评价旨在描述长期生存(≥2 年)、缓解和稳定的特征,并确定有利结果的决定因素。方法:根据 PRISMA 2020 指南进行系统评价。包括确诊 SSPE 病例的研究被认为符合纳入条件。个体患者分析仅限于随访≥2 年的病例。在患者和 队列研究 级别提取数据。结果:对 77 名患者的分析表明,超过 2 年的生存期是可以实现的,但存在差异。生存分布显示,44.2% 的生存时间为 2 至 <5 年,28.6% 的生存时间为 5 至 <10 年,27.3% 的生存时间≥10 年。尽管 62.3% 的患者最初获得缓解或稳定,但 31.2% 的患者后来出现病情进展、严重残疾或死亡。报告的与延长生存期相关的因素包括 治疗 反应性 (62.3%)、疾病进展缓慢 (24.7%) 和复发缓解或双相模式 (20.8%)。其他影响因素包括 宿主 相关因素 (18.2%)、成人发病疾病 (10.4%)、早期 诊断 和脑电图改善。在 31 项 队列研究 研究中,总生存率仍然很低。超过 2 年的生存率报告不一致且差异很大。一些研究观察到病情缓解或稳定,但疾病进展仍然是主要结果。死亡率 在 队列研究 中的范围从低到非常高。报告的与改善生存相关的因素包括疾病进展较慢、发病年龄较大、诊断 处于早期阶段以及使用肌苷普拉诺和干扰素等疗法。结论:SSPE 生存结果显示出明显的异质性。尽管某些患者的生存期延长,但大多数患者的预后较差,这强调了早期 诊断 的重要性以及有效治疗的必要性。

超过 2 年的生存率报告不一致且差异很大。一些研究观察到病情缓解或稳定,但疾病进展仍然是主要结果。死亡率 在 队列研究 中的范围从低到非常高。报告的与改善生存相关的因素包括疾病进展较慢、发病年龄较大、诊断 处于早期阶段以及使用肌苷普拉诺和干扰素等疗法。
科研与实践启示
尽管某些患者的生存期延长,但大多数患者的预后较差,这强调了早期 诊断 的重要性以及有效治疗的必要性。

Long-term survival (≥2 years) in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis: a systematic review of prolonged survivors.

Authors: Ravindra Kumar Garg, Shweta Pandey, Imran Rizvi, Sanjay Singhal
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) is a progressive and often fatal disorder with highly variable survival. This systematic review aimed to characterize long-term survival (≥2 years), remission, and stabilization, and identify determinants of favorable outcomes. METHODS: A systematic review was conducted according to PRISMA 2020 guidelines. Studies including confirmed SSPE cases were considered eligible for inclusion. Individual patient analysis was restricted to cases with ≥2 years of follow-up. Data were extracted at the patient and cohort levels. RESULTS: Analysis of 77 patients demonstrated that survival beyond 2 years is achievable but variable. Survival distribution showed 44.2% surviving 2 to <5 years, 28.6% surviving 5 to <10 years, and 27.3% surviving ≥10 years. Although 62.3% initially achieved remission or stabilization, 31.2% later developed progression, severe disability, or death. Reported factors associated with prolonged survival included treatment responsiveness (62.3%), slow disease progression (24.7%), and relapsing-remitting or biphasic patterns (20.8%). Additional contributors were host-related factors (18.2%), adult-onset disease (10.4%), early diagnosis, and electroencephalographic improvement. Across 31 cohort studies, overall survival remained poor. Survival beyond 2 years was inconsistently reported and varied widely. Remission or stabilization was observed in some studies, but disease progression remained the predominant outcome. Mortality ranged from low to very high across cohorts. Reported factors associated with improved survival included slower disease progression, older age at onset, earlier stage at diagnosis, and use of therapies such as inosine pranobex and interferon. CONCLUSION: SSPE survival outcomes show marked heterogeneity. Although prolonged survival occurs in some, most patients have poor outcomes, emphasizing the importance of early diagnosis and the need for effective therapies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) is a progressive and often fatal disorder with highly variable survival. This systematic review aimed to characterize long-term survival (≥2 years), remission, and stabilization, and identify determinants of favorable outcomes.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review was conducted according to PRISMA 2020 guidelines. Studies including confirmed SSPE cases were considered eligible for inclusion. Individual patient analysis was restricted to cases with ≥2 years of follow-up. Data were extracted at the patient and cohort levels.
Consensus and conclusions
Analysis of 77 patients demonstrated that survival beyond 2 years is achievable but variable. Survival distribution showed 44.2% surviving 2 to <5 years, 28.6% surviving 5 to <10 years, and 27.3% surviving ≥10 years. Although 62.3% initially achieved remission or stabilization, 31.2% later developed progression, severe disability, or death. Reported factors associated with prolonged survival included treatment responsiveness (62.3%), slow disease progression (24.7%), and relapsing-remitting or biphasic patterns (20.8%). Additional contributors were host-related factors (18.2%), adult-onset disease (10.4%), early diagnosis, and electroencephalographic improvement. Across 31 cohort studies, overall survival remained poor.
Controversies and gaps
Survival beyond 2 years was inconsistently reported and varied widely. Remission or stabilization was observed in some studies, but disease progression remained the predominant outcome. Mortality ranged from low to very high across cohorts. Reported factors associated with improved survival included slower disease progression, older age at onset, earlier stage at diagnosis, and use of therapies such as inosine pranobex and interferon.
Research and practice implications
Although prolonged survival occurs in some, most patients have poor outcomes, emphasizing the importance of early diagnosis and the need for effective therapies.
